`packages/db/src/store/principles.test.ts`:

```ts
import { mintId, type KnowledgeTarget, type ProjectId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { findOpenProposalCard, insertInboxItem, updateInboxItem } from './inbox';
import {
  getProposalDto,
  insertDeviation,
  insertProposal,
  insertSource,
  listProposals,
  recentDeviationsFor,
} from './knowledge';
import { searchPrincipleIds, searchPrinciples } from './knowledge-search';
import {
  confirmPrinciple,
  getPrinciple,
  insertPrinciple,
  listPrinciples,
  retirePrinciple,
  withdrawPrinciple,
  type NewPrinciple,
} from './principles';
import type { Store } from './store';
import { createTestStore, seedProject } from './test-support';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let now = new Date('2026-10-22T12:00:00.000Z');
beforeEach(() => {
  now = new Date('2026-10-22T12:00:00.000Z');
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => now));
});
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

const principle = (target: KnowledgeTarget, overrides: Partial<NewPrinciple> = {}) =>
  store.write((ctx) =>
    insertPrinciple(ctx, {
      target,
      statement: 'Never sleep in tests',
      kind: 'policy',
      topicKey: 'testing.no-sleeps',
      status: 'confirmed',
      sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'builder', {}),
      sourceQuote: null,
      speaker: 'me',
      confirmedBy: 'me',
      ...overrides,
    }),
  ).value;

const project = (id: ProjectId) => ({ scope: 'project' as const, projectId: id });
const found = (projectId: ProjectId, query: string) =>
  searchPrinciples(store.db, projectId, query, 10).map((p) => p.statement);

/** A ← B ← C: three confirmed principles with one key and target, each superseding the last. */
const chain = (projectId: ProjectId) => ({
  a: principle(project(projectId), { statement: 'Never sleep in tests' }),
  b: principle(project(projectId), { statement: 'Never sleep in unit tests' }),
  c: principle(project(projectId), { statement: 'Never sleep in any test' }),
});

describe('principles', () => {
  it('reads a confirmed principle back through search, with its key and quote searchable', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const saved = principle(project(p.id), { sourceQuote: 'wait on a condition instead' });
    expect(saved).toMatchObject({ state: 'current', status: 'confirmed', confirmedBy: 'me' });
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual(['Never sleep in tests']);
    expect(found(p.id, 'condition')).toEqual(['Never sleep in tests']);
    expect(found(p.id, 'testing')).toEqual(['Never sleep in tests']);
  });

  it('finds a principle by its edited statement after confirmation, never while proposed', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const proposed = principle(project(p.id), {
      status: 'proposed',
      confirmedBy: null,
      speaker: 'agent:x',
    });
    expect(proposed.state).toBe('proposed');
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual([]);
    expect(
      searchPrincipleIds(store.db, { projectId: p.id, query: 'sleep', limit: 5, history: true }),
    ).toEqual([proposed.id]);
    const confirmed = store.write((ctx) =>
      confirmPrinciple(ctx, proposed.id, {
        target: { scope: 'area', projectId: p.id, area: 'api' },
        statement: 'Never poll in tests',
        topicKey: 'testing.no-polling',
        confirmedBy: 'me',
      }),
    ).value;
    expect(confirmed).toMatchObject({
      state: 'current',
      scope: 'area',
      area: 'api',
      topicKey: 'testing.no-polling',
    });
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual([]);
    expect(found(p.id, 'poll')).toEqual(['Never poll in tests']);
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) =>
        confirmPrinciple(ctx, proposed.id, { target: project(p.id), confirmedBy: 'me' }),
      ),
    ).toThrow(/only a proposed principle/);
  });

  it.each([
    { name: 'the same key and target', second: 'same', supersedes: true },
    { name: 'the same key for you instead of the project', second: 'you', supersedes: false },
    { name: 'the same key in another project', second: 'other-project', supersedes: false },
    { name: 'the same key in another area', second: 'other-area', supersedes: false },
    { name: 'another key, same target', second: 'other-key', supersedes: false },
  ] as const)(
    'supersedes only within the same topic key and target: $name',
    ({ second, supersedes }) => {
      const a = seedProject(store);
      const b = seedProject(store);
      const areaTarget = { scope: 'area' as const, projectId: a.id, area: 'api' };
      const first = principle(second === 'other-area' ? areaTarget : project(a.id));
      const targets = {
        same: project(a.id),
        you: { scope: 'you' as const },
        'other-project': project(b.id),
        'other-area': { scope: 'area' as const, projectId: a.id, area: 'ui' },
        'other-key': project(a.id),
      } as const;
      const next = principle(targets[second], {
        statement: 'Never sleep, even in e2e',
        ...(second === 'other-key' ? { topicKey: 'testing.e2e' } : {}),
      });
      expect(getPrinciple(store.db, first.id).state).toBe(supersedes ? 'superseded' : 'current');
      expect(next.supersedesId).toBe(supersedes ? first.id : null);
      expect(found(a.id, 'sleep').includes('Never sleep in tests')).toBe(!supersedes);
    },
  );

  it('retires a current principle out of search, and refuses to retire a superseded one', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const old = principle(project(p.id));
    const next = principle(project(p.id), { statement: 'Never sleep, ever' });
    expect(() => store.write((ctx) => retirePrinciple(ctx, old.id, 'me', 'gone'))).toThrow(
      /only a current principle/,
    );
    const retired = store.write((ctx) =>
      retirePrinciple(ctx, next.id, 'me', 'we use fake timers now'),
    ).value;
    expect(retired).toMatchObject({ state: 'retired', retiredReason: 'we use fake timers now' });
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual([]);
    expect(
      listPrinciples(store.db, { projectId: p.id, history: true }).map((x) => x.state),
    ).toEqual(['retired', 'superseded']);
  });

  it('never returns a proposed, retired, superseded or withdrawn principle from a current search', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    principle(project(p.id), { status: 'proposed', confirmedBy: null, topicKey: 'a' });
    const retired = principle(project(p.id), { topicKey: 'b' });
    store.write((ctx) => retirePrinciple(ctx, retired.id, 'me', 'old'));
    principle(project(p.id), { topicKey: 'c' });
    principle(project(p.id), { topicKey: 'c', statement: 'Sleep only in the e2e harness' });
    const withdrawn = principle(project(p.id), { topicKey: 'd' });
    store.write((ctx) => withdrawPrinciple(ctx, withdrawn.id, 'me', 'typo'));
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual(['Sleep only in the e2e harness']);
    expect(listPrinciples(store.db, { projectId: p.id }).map((x) => x.topicKey)).toEqual(['c']);
  });

  it('makes only the link before the head current when the head of a three-link chain is withdrawn', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const { a, b, c } = chain(p.id);
    store.write((ctx) => withdrawPrinciple(ctx, c.id, 'me', 'reverted'));
    expect([a, b, c].map((x) => getPrinciple(store.db, x.id).state)).toEqual([
      'superseded',
      'current',
      'withdrawn',
    ]);
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual(['Never sleep in unit tests']);
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'withdrawing a superseded record is refused (the middle of a three-link chain)',
      before: 'none',
      refused: 'b',
      current: ['Never sleep in any test'],
    },
    {
      name: 'withdrawing a superseded record is refused (the root of a three-link chain)',
      before: 'none',
      refused: 'a',
      current: ['Never sleep in any test'],
    },
    {
      name: 'withdrawing a withdrawn record is refused',
      before: 'withdraw',
      refused: 'c',
      current: ['Never sleep in unit tests'],
    },
    {
      name: 'withdrawing a retired record is refused',
      before: 'retire',
      refused: 'c',
      current: [],
    },
  ] as const)('$name, and at most one link stays current', ({ before, refused, current }) => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const links = chain(p.id);
    if (before === 'withdraw')
      store.write((ctx) => withdrawPrinciple(ctx, links.c.id, 'me', 'reverted'));
    if (before === 'retire')
      store.write((ctx) => retirePrinciple(ctx, links.c.id, 'me', 'fake timers now'));
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) => withdrawPrinciple(ctx, links[refused].id, 'me', 'no longer true')),
    ).toThrow(/only a current or proposed principle can be withdrawn/);
    expect(found(p.id, 'sleep')).toEqual(current);
  });

  it('filters the list by scope, area and kind', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    principle({ scope: 'you' }, { topicKey: 'mine', kind: 'heuristic' });
    principle(project(p.id), { topicKey: 'ours' });
    principle(
      { scope: 'area', projectId: p.id, area: 'api' },
      { topicKey: 'api-only', kind: 'belief' },
    );
    const keys = (filter: Omit<Parameters<typeof listPrinciples>[1], 'projectId'>) =>
      listPrinciples(store.db, { projectId: p.id, ...filter })
        .map((x) => x.topicKey)
        .sort();
    expect(keys({})).toEqual(['api-only', 'mine', 'ours']);
    expect(keys({ scope: 'you' })).toEqual(['mine']);
    expect(keys({ scope: 'area', area: 'api' })).toEqual(['api-only']);
    expect(keys({ kind: 'policy' })).toEqual(['ours']);
  });
});

describe('principle proposals and deviations', () => {
  it('lists a pending proposal with its principle and finds its open card', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const proposed = principle(project(p.id), { status: 'proposed', confirmedBy: null });
    const proposalId = store.write((ctx) =>
      insertProposal(ctx, {
        projectId: p.id,
        kind: 'principle',
        title: 'No sleeps',
        body: 'b',
        sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'agent', {}),
        principleId: proposed.id,
      }),
    ).value;
    expect(listProposals(store.db, { projectId: p.id, status: 'pending' })).toEqual([
      getProposalDto(store.db, proposalId),
    ]);
    expect(getProposalDto(store.db, proposalId).principle?.id).toBe(proposed.id);
    const card = store.write((ctx) =>
      insertInboxItem(ctx, {
        projectId: p.id,
        kind: 'proposal',
        title: 'No sleeps',
        body: '',
        options: [{ label: 'reject', description: '' }],
        recommendation: null,
        category: 'product',
        urgency: 'low',
        detail: { proposalId },
        foldKey: null,
        targets: [],
      }),
    ).value;
    expect(findOpenProposalCard(store.db, proposalId)?.id).toBe(card.id);
    store.write((ctx) =>
      updateInboxItem(ctx, card.id, { state: 'RESOLVED', answer: { choice: 'reject' } }),
    );
    expect(findOpenProposalCard(store.db, proposalId)).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('counts the deviations that name a principle since a time', () => {
    const p = seedProject(store);
    const rule = principle(project(p.id));
    const deviate = () =>
      store.write((ctx) =>
        insertDeviation(ctx, {
          projectId: p.id,
          workstreamId: mintId('Workstream'),
          cause: 'slept',
          severity: 'warn',
          signedReason: 'flaky upstream (agent:x)',
          sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'agent', {}),
          principleId: rule.id,
        }),
      );
    deviate();
    now = new Date('2026-11-25T12:00:00.000Z');
    deviate();
    expect(recentDeviationsFor(store.db, rule.id, '2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(
      recentDeviationsFor(store.db, rule.id, '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z').map((d) => d.signedReason),
    ).toEqual(['flaky upstream (agent:x)', 'flaky upstream (agent:x)']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the principles repository**

`packages/db/src/store/principles.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ConflictError,
  EventType,
  KnowledgeState,
  mintId,
  NotFoundError,
  PrincipleDto,
  PrincipleStatus,
  targetColumns,
  type KnowledgeScope,
  type KnowledgeTarget,
  type PrincipleId,
  type PrincipleKind,
  type ProjectId,
  type SourceId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { eq, sql, type SQL } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Json } from '../json';
import * as schema from '../schema';
import type { DbReader, WriteContext } from './store';

/** A confirmed successor of `p`: it names `p`, isn't withdrawn and was confirmed (spec §4). */
const SUCCESSOR = sql`(SELECT s.id FROM principles s WHERE s.supersedes_id = p.id AND s.withdrawn_at IS NULL AND s.status <> ${PrincipleStatus.Proposed} ORDER BY s.id DESC LIMIT 1)`;

/** True for a current principle: confirmed, not withdrawn, not superseded. */
export const currentPrincipleSql = sql`(p.withdrawn_at IS NULL AND p.status = ${PrincipleStatus.Confirmed} AND ${SUCCESSOR} IS NULL)`;

// Column aliases match PrincipleDto, so each row parses as one; the state is derived here, never stored.
const SELECT_PRINCIPLE = sql`
  SELECT p.id AS id, p.project_id AS projectId, p.scope AS scope, p.area AS area,
    p.statement AS statement, p.kind AS kind, p.topic_key AS topicKey, p.status AS status,
    CASE WHEN p.withdrawn_at IS NOT NULL THEN ${KnowledgeState.Withdrawn}
         WHEN p.status = ${PrincipleStatus.Proposed} THEN ${KnowledgeState.Proposed}
         WHEN ${SUCCESSOR} IS NOT NULL THEN ${KnowledgeState.Superseded}
         WHEN p.status = ${PrincipleStatus.Retired} THEN ${KnowledgeState.Retired}
         ELSE ${KnowledgeState.Current} END AS state,
    p.supersedes_id AS supersedesId, ${SUCCESSOR} AS supersededById, p.source_id AS sourceId,
    p.source_quote AS sourceQuote, p.speaker AS speaker, p.confirmed_by AS confirmedBy,
    p.confirmed_at AS confirmedAt, p.retired_at AS retiredAt, p.retired_reason AS retiredReason,
    p.withdrawn_at AS withdrawnAt, p.withdrawn_reason AS withdrawnReason,
    p.created_at AS createdAt, p.updated_at AS updatedAt
  FROM principles p`;

function select(db: DbReader, where: SQL): PrincipleDto[] {
  return PrincipleDto.array().parse(
    db.all(sql`${SELECT_PRINCIPLE} WHERE ${where} ORDER BY p.id DESC`),
  );
}

function saved(ctx: WriteContext, id: PrincipleId, data: Record<string, Json>): void {
  ctx.emit({ type: EventType.PrincipleSaved, entityType: 'principle', entityId: id, data });
}

export function getPrinciple(db: DbReader, id: PrincipleId): PrincipleDto {
  const row = select(db, sql`p.id = ${id}`)[0];
  if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('principle', id);
  return row;
}

/** The current principle with this key at exactly this target, if any. */
export function currentPrincipleFor(
  db: DbReader,
  topicKey: string,
  target: KnowledgeTarget,
): PrincipleDto | undefined {
  const c = targetColumns(target);
  return select(
    db,
    sql`p.topic_key = ${topicKey} AND p.scope = ${c.scope} AND p.project_id IS ${c.projectId} AND p.area IS ${c.area} AND ${currentPrincipleSql}`,
  )[0];
}

export interface NewPrinciple {
  readonly target: KnowledgeTarget;
  readonly statement: string;
  readonly kind: PrincipleKind;
  readonly topicKey: string;
  readonly status: typeof PrincipleStatus.Proposed | typeof PrincipleStatus.Confirmed;
  readonly sourceId: SourceId;
  readonly sourceQuote: string | null;
  /** Who stated it: `me`, or `agent:<session>`. */
  readonly speaker: string | null;
  readonly confirmedBy: string | null;
}

/** Inserts a principle; a confirmed one supersedes the current one with its key and target (spec §4). */
export function insertPrinciple(ctx: WriteContext, input: NewPrinciple): PrincipleDto {
  const id = mintId('Principle');
  const confirmed = input.status === PrincipleStatus.Confirmed;
  const previous = confirmed
    ? currentPrincipleFor(ctx.tx, input.topicKey, input.target)
    : undefined;
  ctx.tx
    .insert(schema.principles)
    .values({
      id,
      ...targetColumns(input.target),
      statement: input.statement,
      kind: input.kind,
      topicKey: input.topicKey,
      status: input.status,
      supersedesId: previous?.id ?? null,
      sourceId: input.sourceId,
      sourceQuote: input.sourceQuote,
      speaker: input.speaker,
      confirmedBy: confirmed ? input.confirmedBy : null,
      confirmedAt: confirmed ? ctx.now : null,
      createdAt: ctx.now,
      updatedAt: ctx.now,
    })
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, { status: input.status, topicKey: input.topicKey });
  if (previous) saved(ctx, previous.id, { supersededBy: id });
  return getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
}

export interface ConfirmPrinciple {
  readonly target: KnowledgeTarget;
  readonly statement?: string | undefined;
  readonly kind?: PrincipleKind | undefined;
  readonly topicKey?: string | undefined;
  readonly confirmedBy: string;
}

/** Confirms a proposed principle at a target, with any edits; it supersedes like an insert does. */
export function confirmPrinciple(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: PrincipleId,
  input: ConfirmPrinciple,
): PrincipleDto {
  const current = getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
  if (current.state !== KnowledgeState.Proposed)
    throw new ConflictError(
      `Principle ${id} is ${current.state}; only a proposed principle can be confirmed`,
    );
  const topicKey = input.topicKey ?? current.topicKey;
  const previous = currentPrincipleFor(ctx.tx, topicKey, input.target);
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.principles)
    .set({
      ...targetColumns(input.target),
      statement: input.statement ?? current.statement,
      kind: input.kind ?? current.kind,
      topicKey,
      status: PrincipleStatus.Confirmed,
      supersedesId: previous?.id ?? null,
      confirmedBy: input.confirmedBy,
      confirmedAt: ctx.now,
      updatedAt: ctx.now,
    })
    .where(eq(schema.principles.id, id))
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, { status: PrincipleStatus.Confirmed, topicKey });
  if (previous) saved(ctx, previous.id, { supersededBy: id });
  return getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
}

export function retirePrinciple(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: PrincipleId,
  by: string,
  reason: string,
): PrincipleDto {
  const current = getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
  if (current.state !== KnowledgeState.Current)
    throw new ConflictError(
      `Principle ${id} is ${current.state}; only a current principle can be retired`,
    );
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.principles)
    .set({
      status: PrincipleStatus.Retired,
      retiredAt: ctx.now,
      retiredBy: by,
      retiredReason: reason,
      updatedAt: ctx.now,
    })
    .where(eq(schema.principles.id, id))
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, { status: PrincipleStatus.Retired, by });
  return getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
}

/**
 * Withdraws a current principle, or a proposed one when its proposal is rejected: the row stays as a
 * tombstone with who and why (spec §9). Only the head of a chain can go; withdrawing a superseded link
 * would make its predecessor current beside its successor, two current principles under one key.
 */
export function withdrawPrinciple(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: PrincipleId,
  by: string,
  reason: string,
): PrincipleDto {
  const current = getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
  if (current.state !== KnowledgeState.Current && current.state !== KnowledgeState.Proposed)
    throw new ConflictError(
      `Principle ${id} is ${current.state}; only a current or proposed principle can be withdrawn`,
    );
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.principles)
    .set({ withdrawnAt: ctx.now, withdrawnBy: by, withdrawnReason: reason, updatedAt: ctx.now })
    .where(eq(schema.principles.id, id))
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, { withdrawn: true, by });
  return getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id);
}

export interface PrincipleFilter {
  readonly projectId: ProjectId;
  readonly scope?: KnowledgeScope;
  readonly area?: string;
  readonly kind?: PrincipleKind;
  /** Also proposed, superseded, retired and withdrawn principles. */
  readonly history?: boolean;
  /** Only these, in this order. */
  readonly ids?: readonly PrincipleId[];
}

/** The project's principles and the builder's own, newest first (or in `ids` order); current ones unless `history`. */
export function listPrinciples(db: DbReader, filter: PrincipleFilter): PrincipleDto[] {
  if (filter.ids?.length === 0) return [];
  const where: SQL[] = [sql`(p.project_id = ${filter.projectId} OR p.project_id IS NULL)`];
  if (filter.scope) where.push(sql`p.scope = ${filter.scope}`);
  if (filter.area) where.push(sql`p.area = ${filter.area}`);
  if (filter.kind) where.push(sql`p.kind = ${filter.kind}`);
  if (!filter.history) where.push(currentPrincipleSql);
  if (filter.ids)
    where.push(
      sql`p.id IN (${sql.join(
        filter.ids.map((id) => sql`${id}`),
        sql`, `,
      )})`,
    );
  const rows = select(db, sql.join(where, sql` AND `));
  if (!filter.ids) return rows;
  const order = new Map(filter.ids.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
  return rows.toSorted((a, b) => (order.get(a.id) ?? 0) - (order.get(b.id) ?? 0));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Search principles, and link proposals and deviations**

Append to `packages/db/src/store/knowledge-search.ts` (add `PrincipleId`, `PrincipleDto` and `PrincipleKind` to the contracts import and `currentPrincipleSql`, `listPrinciples` from `./principles`):

```ts
const PrincipleIdRows = z.array(z.object({ id: PrincipleId }));

/** Principles matching the query, best first; current ones unless `history`. */
export function searchPrincipleIds(
  db: DbReader,
  q: SearchQuery & { readonly kind?: PrincipleKind },
): PrincipleId[] {
  const match = ftsQuery(q.query);
  if (match === null) return [];
  const where = [sql`principles_fts MATCH ${match}`, ...narrow('p', q)];
  if (q.kind) where.push(sql`p.kind = ${q.kind}`);
  if (!q.history) where.push(currentPrincipleSql);
  const rows = db.all(sql`
    SELECT p.id AS id FROM principles_fts f JOIN principles p ON p.rowid = f.rowid
    WHERE ${sql.join(where, sql` AND `)}
    ORDER BY bm25(principles_fts), p.id
    LIMIT ${q.limit}`);
  return PrincipleIdRows.parse(rows).map((r) => r.id);
}

/** The current principles in scope for the project that match `query`, best first. */
export function searchPrinciples(
  db: DbReader,
  projectId: ProjectId,
  query: string,
  limit: number,
): PrincipleDto[] {
  return listPrinciples(db, {
    projectId,
    ids: searchPrincipleIds(db, { projectId, query, limit }),
  });
}
```

- Add:

```ts
function toProposalDto(db: DbReader, row: ProposalRow): ProposalDto {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    projectId: row.projectId,
    kind: row.kind,
    title: row.title,
    body: row.body,
    status: row.status,
    rationale: row.rationale,
    principle: row.principleId ? getPrinciple(db, row.principleId) : null,
    sourceId: row.sourceId,
    createdAt: row.createdAt,
    updatedAt: row.updatedAt,
  };
}

export function getProposalDto(db: DbReader, id: ProposalId): ProposalDto {
  return toProposalDto(db, getProposal(db, id));
}

/** A project's proposals in one status, oldest first, as the inbox orders them. */
export function listProposals(
  db: DbReader,
  filter: { readonly projectId: ProjectId; readonly status: ProposalStatus },
): ProposalDto[] {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(schema.proposals)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(schema.proposals.projectId, filter.projectId),
        eq(schema.proposals.status, filter.status),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(asc(schema.proposals.id))
    .all()
    .map((row) => toProposalDto(db, row));
}

/** Deviations that name the principle, recorded at or after `since`, oldest first. */
export function recentDeviationsFor(
  db: DbReader,
  principleId: PrincipleId,
  since: string,
): { readonly id: DeviationId; readonly signedReason: string }[] {
  return db
    .select({ id: schema.deviations.id, signedReason: schema.deviations.signedReason })
    .from(schema.deviations)
    .where(
      and(eq(schema.deviations.principleId, principleId), gte(schema.deviations.createdAt, since)),
    )
    .orderBy(asc(schema.deviations.id))
    .all();
}
```

In `packages/db/src/store/inbox.ts`, add (with `InboxKind` and `ProposalId` imported):

```ts
/** The open card that asks the builder about a proposal, if it is still open. */
export function findOpenProposalCard(
  db: DbReader,
  proposalId: ProposalId,
): InboxItemDto | undefined {
  const row = db
    .select()
    .from(schema.inboxItems)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(schema.inboxItems.kind, InboxKind.Proposal),
        eq(schema.inboxItems.state, InboxState.Open),
        sql`json_extract(${schema.inboxItems.detail}, '$.proposalId') = ${proposalId}`,
      ),
    )
    .get();
  return row ? toDto(row) : undefined;
}
```

### Task 5: The knowledge module: scope and the packer

Pure rules, no I/O: which records apply to a task, which principle wins for a topic key, and how the pack fills its budget.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/knowledge/scope.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/knowledge/pack.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/knowledge/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/knowledge/pack.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/AGENTS.md` (the module and its tier)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `KnowledgeScope`, `PrincipleKind`, `DecisionId`, `PrincipleId` (contracts only). Records the builder signs use the existing `BUILDER_HANDLE` (`'me'`) from `@10db/db`, never a new constant.
- Produces:
  - `scope.ts`: `Scoped` (`{ scope: KnowledgeScope; area: string | null }`), `mostSpecific<T extends Scoped & { topicKey: string }>(records: readonly T[], areas: readonly string[]): T[]` (kind-blind; callers filter kinds first). `specificity(scope): number` (used by `pack.ts`) and `appliesTo(record: Scoped, areas: readonly string[]): boolean` (used by `pack.ts` and the test) are exported from the file for those callers only; `knowledge/index.ts` doesn't re-export them.
  - `pack.ts`: `CONTEXT_PACK_BUDGET_TOKENS = 1500`, `CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4`, `PACK_SEARCH_LIMIT = 20`, `PACK_RECENT_LIMIT = 5`, `PackPrinciple`, `PackDecision`, `PackInput`, `PackShelves` (`{ policies; heuristics; decisions }`), `KnowledgePack`, `principleTokens(p): number`, `decisionTokens(d): number`, `packKnowledge(input: PackInput): KnowledgePack`.

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/pack.test.ts`:

```ts
import { mintId, type DecisionId, type KnowledgeScope, type PrincipleKind } from '@10db/contracts';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { appliesTo, mostSpecific } from './scope';
import {
  decisionTokens,
  packKnowledge,
  principleTokens,
  type PackDecision,
  type PackInput,
  type PackPrinciple,
} from './pack';

const rule = (
  topicKey: string,
  kind: PrincipleKind = 'policy',
  scope: KnowledgeScope = 'project',
  area: string | null = null,
  statement = `Rule ${topicKey}`,
): PackPrinciple => ({ id: mintId('Principle'), statement, kind, topicKey, scope, area });

const decided = (
  question: string,
  scope: KnowledgeScope = 'project',
  area: string | null = null,
): PackDecision => ({ id: mintId('Decision'), question, choice: 'c', rationale: 'r', scope, area });

const input = (over: Partial<PackInput> = {}): PackInput => ({
  areas: ['api'],
  principles: [],
  decisions: [],
  ranked: [],
  budgetTokens: 1500,
  ...over,
});

describe('scope', () => {
  it.each([
    { name: 'yours', scope: 'you', area: null, applies: true },
    { name: 'the project', scope: 'project', area: null, applies: true },
    { name: 'an area the task works in', scope: 'area', area: 'api', applies: true },
    { name: 'another area', scope: 'area', area: 'ui', applies: false },
  ] as const)('a record for $name applies: $applies', ({ scope, area, applies }) => {
    expect(appliesTo({ scope, area }, ['api'])).toBe(applies);
  });

  it.each([
    { name: 'project over you', rules: [rule('k', 'policy', 'you'), rule('k')], kept: ['project'] },
    {
      name: 'area over project',
      rules: [rule('k'), rule('k', 'policy', 'area', 'api')],
      kept: ['area'],
    },
    {
      name: 'project over an area the task is not in',
      rules: [rule('k'), rule('k', 'policy', 'area', 'ui')],
      kept: ['project'],
    },
    {
      name: 'two different keys',
      rules: [rule('a', 'policy', 'you'), rule('b')],
      kept: ['you', 'project'],
    },
    {
      name: 'an area belief over a project policy, since it ignores kind (the packer drops beliefs first)',
      rules: [rule('k'), rule('k', 'belief', 'area', 'api')],
      kept: ['area'],
    },
  ])('keeps only the most specific principle per topic key: $name', ({ rules, kept }) => {
    expect(mostSpecific(rules, ['api']).map((r) => r.scope)).toEqual(kept);
  });
});

describe('packKnowledge', () => {
  it('says nothing applies when there is nothing', () => {
    expect(packKnowledge(input())).toEqual({
      policies: [],
      heuristics: [],
      decisions: [],
      budgetTokens: 1500,
      usedTokens: 0,
      dropped: { policies: 0, heuristics: 0, decisions: 0 },
    });
  });

  it('packs policies, then heuristics, area before project before you, and never beliefs', () => {
    const pack = packKnowledge(
      input({
        principles: [
          rule('mine', 'policy', 'you'),
          rule('ours', 'policy'),
          rule('api', 'policy', 'area', 'api'),
          rule('prefer', 'heuristic'),
          rule('context', 'belief'),
          rule('ui', 'policy', 'area', 'ui'),
          rule('ours', 'belief', 'area', 'api'),
          rule('prefer', 'belief', 'area', 'api'),
        ],
      }),
    );
    expect(pack.policies.map((p) => p.topicKey)).toEqual(['api', 'ours', 'mine']);
    expect(pack.policies.find((p) => p.topicKey === 'ours')?.scope).toBe('project');
    expect(pack.heuristics.map((p) => p.topicKey)).toEqual(['prefer']);
    expect(pack.dropped).toEqual({ policies: 0, heuristics: 0, decisions: 0 });
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'an area belief and a project policy with one key',
      principles: [rule('k', 'belief', 'area', 'api'), rule('k', 'policy')],
      policies: ['project'],
      heuristics: [],
    },
    {
      name: 'a project belief and a heuristic of yours with one key',
      principles: [rule('k', 'belief'), rule('k', 'heuristic', 'you')],
      policies: [],
      heuristics: ['you'],
    },
  ])('never lets a belief hide a rule: $name', ({ principles, policies, heuristics }) => {
    const pack = packKnowledge(input({ principles }));
    expect(pack.policies.map((p) => p.scope)).toEqual(policies);
    expect(pack.heuristics.map((p) => p.scope)).toEqual(heuristics);
  });

  it('orders decisions: area hits, other hits by rank, then area decisions without a hit', () => {
    const projectHit = decided('Which queue?');
    const areaHit = decided('Which router?', 'area', 'api');
    const areaOther = decided('Which validator?', 'area', 'api');
    const uiHit = decided('Which icons?', 'area', 'ui');
    const unrelated = decided('Which font?');
    const pack = packKnowledge(
      input({
        decisions: [unrelated, uiHit, areaOther, areaHit, projectHit],
        ranked: [projectHit.id, uiHit.id, areaHit.id],
      }),
    );
    expect(pack.decisions.map((d) => d.question)).toEqual([
      'Which router?',
      'Which queue?',
      'Which validator?',
    ]);
  });

  it('falls back to the newest decisions that apply when nothing matches the task', () => {
    const decisions = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => decided(`Q${String(i)}`));
    const pack = packKnowledge(
      input({ decisions: [decided('UI only', 'area', 'ui'), ...decisions] }),
    );
    expect(pack.decisions.map((d) => d.question)).toEqual(['Q0', 'Q1', 'Q2', 'Q3', 'Q4']);
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'a policy longer than the budget is skipped and the next one still fits',
      principles: [rule('big', 'policy', 'project', null, 'x'.repeat(400)), rule('small')],
      budget: 50,
      kept: ['small'],
      dropped: { policies: 1, heuristics: 0, decisions: 0 },
    },
    {
      name: 'policies fill the budget first and heuristics are counted as dropped',
      principles: [
        rule('a', 'policy', 'project', null, 'x'.repeat(150)),
        rule('b', 'heuristic', 'project', null, 'y'.repeat(150)),
      ],
      budget: 45,
      kept: ['a'],
      dropped: { policies: 0, heuristics: 1, decisions: 0 },
    },
    {
      name: 'a zero budget drops everything and says so',
      principles: [rule('a'), rule('b', 'heuristic')],
      budget: 0,
      kept: [],
      dropped: { policies: 1, heuristics: 1, decisions: 0 },
    },
  ])('fills the budget greedily: $name', ({ principles, budget, kept, dropped }) => {
    const pack = packKnowledge(input({ principles, budgetTokens: budget }));
    expect([...pack.policies, ...pack.heuristics].map((p) => p.topicKey)).toEqual(kept);
    expect(pack.dropped).toEqual(dropped);
    expect(pack.usedTokens).toBeLessThanOrEqual(budget);
    expect(pack.usedTokens).toBe(
      [...pack.policies, ...pack.heuristics].reduce((n, p) => n + principleTokens(p), 0) +
        pack.decisions.reduce((n, d) => n + decisionTokens(d), 0),
    );
  });

  it('is deterministic whatever order the rows arrive in', () => {
    const principles = [rule('b'), rule('a'), rule('c', 'heuristic'), rule('a', 'policy', 'you')];
    const decisions = [decided('One'), decided('Two')];
    const ranked: DecisionId[] = decisions.map((d) => d.id);
    const first = packKnowledge(input({ principles, decisions, ranked }));
    expect(
      packKnowledge(input({ principles: principles.toReversed(), decisions, ranked })),
    ).toEqual(first);
    expect(first.policies.map((p) => p.topicKey)).toEqual(['a', 'b']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the scope rules**

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/scope.ts`:

```ts
import { KnowledgeScope } from '@10db/contracts';

export interface Scoped {
  readonly scope: KnowledgeScope;
  readonly area: string | null;
}

/** An area rule is more specific than a project rule, which is more specific than the builder's own. */
export function specificity(scope: KnowledgeScope): number {
  switch (scope) {
    case KnowledgeScope.You:
      return 0;
    case KnowledgeScope.Project:
      return 1;
    case KnowledgeScope.Area:
      return 2;
  }
}

/**
 * Whether a record of the task's project (or one of the builder's own) applies to work in `areas`.
 * Callers pass only records of that project and the builder's own.
 */
export function appliesTo(record: Scoped, areas: readonly string[]): boolean {
  switch (record.scope) {
    case KnowledgeScope.You:
    case KnowledgeScope.Project:
      return true;
    case KnowledgeScope.Area:
      return record.area !== null && areas.includes(record.area);
  }
}

/**
 * For each topic key, the applying records of the highest specificity. It ignores kind, so callers
 * drop the kinds they don't want first: the packer drops beliefs, so a belief never hides a rule.
 */
export function mostSpecific<T extends Scoped & { readonly topicKey: string }>(
  records: readonly T[],
  areas: readonly string[],
): T[] {
  const applying = records.filter((r) => appliesTo(r, areas));
  const best = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of applying)
    best.set(r.topicKey, Math.max(best.get(r.topicKey) ?? -1, specificity(r.scope)));
  return applying.filter((r) => specificity(r.scope) === best.get(r.topicKey));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the packer and the module's entry**

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/pack.ts`:

```ts
import { KnowledgeScope, PrincipleKind, type DecisionId, type PrincipleId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { appliesTo, mostSpecific, specificity } from './scope';

/** Kept small: an always-loaded core memory that grew failed a held-out eval (spec §9). */
export const CONTEXT_PACK_BUDGET_TOKENS = 1500;
/** A rough estimate that needs no tokenizer; the budget is a ceiling, not a measurement. */
export const CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;
/** How many search hits for the task are considered. */
export const PACK_SEARCH_LIMIT = 20;
/** How many recent decisions stand in when nothing matches the task. */
export const PACK_RECENT_LIMIT = 5;

export interface PackPrinciple {
  readonly id: PrincipleId;
  readonly statement: string;
  readonly kind: PrincipleKind;
  readonly topicKey: string;
  readonly scope: KnowledgeScope;
  readonly area: string | null;
}

export interface PackDecision {
  readonly id: DecisionId;
  readonly question: string;
  readonly choice: string;
  readonly rationale: string;
  readonly scope: KnowledgeScope;
  readonly area: string | null;
}

export interface PackInput {
  /** The areas the task lists plus the areas the workstream holds live claims on. */
  readonly areas: readonly string[];
  /** Current confirmed principles of the project and the builder's own. */
  readonly principles: readonly PackPrinciple[];
  /** Current decisions of the project and the builder's own, newest first. */
  readonly decisions: readonly PackDecision[];
  /** Search hits for the task among those decisions, best first. */
  readonly ranked: readonly DecisionId[];
  readonly budgetTokens: number;
}

export interface PackShelves {
  readonly policies: number;
  readonly heuristics: number;
  readonly decisions: number;
}

export interface KnowledgePack {
  readonly policies: readonly PackPrinciple[];
  readonly heuristics: readonly PackPrinciple[];
  readonly decisions: readonly PackDecision[];
  readonly budgetTokens: number;
  readonly usedTokens: number;
  readonly dropped: PackShelves;
}

// The fixed overheads cover the bullet, the key's brackets and the arrow and parentheses of a rendered line.
export const principleTokens = (p: PackPrinciple): number =>
  Math.ceil((p.topicKey.length + p.statement.length + 8) / CHARS_PER_TOKEN);
export const decisionTokens = (d: PackDecision): number =>
  Math.ceil((d.question.length + d.choice.length + d.rationale.length + 16) / CHARS_PER_TOKEN);

const compareText = (a: string, b: string): number => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);

const byPrecedence = (a: PackPrinciple, b: PackPrinciple): number =>
  specificity(b.scope) - specificity(a.scope) ||
  compareText(a.topicKey, b.topicKey) ||
  compareText(a.id, b.id);

/** Area-matched hits, other hits by rank, then area decisions without a hit; else the newest that apply. */
function relevantDecisions(input: PackInput): PackDecision[] {
  const applying = input.decisions.filter((d) => appliesTo(d, input.areas));
  const byId = new Map(applying.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
  const hits = input.ranked.flatMap((id) => {
    const d = byId.get(id);
    return d ? [d] : [];
  });
  const hitIds = new Set(hits.map((d) => d.id));
  const ordered = [
    ...hits.filter((d) => d.scope === KnowledgeScope.Area),
    ...hits.filter((d) => d.scope !== KnowledgeScope.Area),
    ...applying.filter((d) => d.scope === KnowledgeScope.Area && !hitIds.has(d.id)),
  ];
  return ordered.length > 0 ? ordered : applying.slice(0, PACK_RECENT_LIMIT);
}

/** Keeps each item that still fits, in order, skipping one that doesn't (the next may be smaller). */
function fill<T>(items: readonly T[], size: (item: T) => number, room: { left: number }) {
  const kept: T[] = [];
  for (const item of items) {
    const n = size(item);
    if (n > room.left) continue;
    kept.push(item);
    room.left -= n;
  }
  return { kept, dropped: items.length - kept.length };
}

/**
 * The knowledge part of the context pack (spec §9): confirmed policies in scope, then heuristics, then
 * the decisions relevant to the task, filled greedily into the budget. Beliefs are context for
 * find_knowledge, not rules, so they are not packed; they are dropped before the most specific record
 * per key is chosen, so an area belief can't hide a project policy. Deterministic for the same rows in
 * any order.
 */
export function packKnowledge(input: PackInput): KnowledgePack {
  const rules = input.principles.filter((p) => p.kind !== PrincipleKind.Belief);
  const principles = mostSpecific(rules, input.areas).toSorted(byPrecedence);
  const room = { left: input.budgetTokens };
  const policies = fill(
    principles.filter((p) => p.kind === PrincipleKind.Policy),
    principleTokens,
    room,
  );
  const heuristics = fill(
    principles.filter((p) => p.kind === PrincipleKind.Heuristic),
    principleTokens,
    room,
  );
  const decisions = fill(relevantDecisions(input), decisionTokens, room);
  return {
    policies: policies.kept,
    heuristics: heuristics.kept,
    decisions: decisions.kept,
    budgetTokens: input.budgetTokens,
    usedTokens: input.budgetTokens - room.left,
    dropped: {
      policies: policies.dropped,
      heuristics: heuristics.dropped,
      decisions: decisions.dropped,
    },
  };
}
```

Replace the `describe('buildContextPack', …)` block with:

```ts
describe('buildContextPack', () => {
  let close: () => void;
  let store: ReturnType<typeof createTestStore>['store'];
  beforeEach(() => ({ store, close } = createTestStore()));
  afterEach(() => {
    close();
  });

  const recordDecision = (
    projectId: Parameters<typeof insertDecision>[1]['projectId'],
    question: string,
    over: Partial<Parameters<typeof insertDecision>[1]> = {},
  ) =>
    store.write((ctx) =>
      insertDecision(ctx, {
        projectId,
        question,
        choice: 'x'.repeat(300),
        rationale: 'r',
        decidedBy: 'me',
        scope: 'project',
        area: null,
        sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'builder', {}),
        ...over,
      }),
    ).value;

  const rule = (
    target: NewPrinciple['target'],
    topicKey: string,
    over: Partial<NewPrinciple> = {},
  ) =>
    store.write((ctx) =>
      insertPrinciple(ctx, {
        target,
        statement: `Rule ${topicKey}`,
        kind: 'policy',
        topicKey,
        status: 'confirmed',
        sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'builder', {}),
        sourceQuote: null,
        speaker: 'me',
        confirmedBy: 'me',
        ...over,
      }),
    ).value;

  const keys = (pack: ContextPack) =>
    [...pack.knowledge.policies, ...pack.knowledge.heuristics].map((p) => p.topicKey);

  it('says so when there is no knowledge', () => {
    const { workstream } = seedWorkstream(store);
    const pack = buildContextPack(store.db, workstream);
    expect(pack.knowledge).toEqual(emptyPack.knowledge);
    expect(pack.task.title).toBe('Alpha');
  });

  it('packs only records that apply to this project and the task’s areas', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { areas: ['api'] });
    const other = seedWorkstream(store, { title: 'Other' }).project;
    rule({ scope: 'you' }, 'mine');
    rule({ scope: 'project', projectId: project.id }, 'ours');
    rule({ scope: 'area', projectId: project.id, area: 'api' }, 'api-rule');
    rule({ scope: 'area', projectId: project.id, area: 'ui' }, 'ui-rule');
    rule({ scope: 'project', projectId: other.id }, 'theirs');
    recordDecision(project.id, 'Which icons for the ui?', {
      scope: 'area',
      area: 'ui',
      choice: 'tabler',
    });
    const pack = buildContextPack(store.db, workstream);
    expect(keys(pack)).toEqual(['api-rule', 'ours', 'mine']);
    expect(pack.knowledge.decisions).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('widens the areas to a live area claim', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { areas: ['api'] });
    rule({ scope: 'area', projectId: project.id, area: 'ui' }, 'ui-rule');
    store.write((ctx) =>
      acquireClaims(ctx, {
        projectId: project.id,
        workstreamId: workstream.id,
        requests: [{ kind: 'area', name: 'ui' }],
        leaseExpiresAt: ctx.now,
      }),
    );
    expect(keys(buildContextPack(store.db, workstream))).toEqual(['ui-rule']);
  });

  it('packs no superseded, withdrawn, retired or proposed record', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { title: 'Alpha queue' });
    const target = { scope: 'project' as const, projectId: project.id };
    rule(target, 'a', { statement: 'Old a' });
    rule(target, 'a', { statement: 'New a' });
    const retired = rule(target, 'b');
    store.write((ctx) => retirePrinciple(ctx, retired.id, 'me', 'old'));
    rule(target, 'c', { status: 'proposed', confirmedBy: null });
    const old = recordDecision(project.id, 'Which queue?', { choice: 'BullMQ' });
    store.write((ctx) =>
      supersedeDecision(ctx, old, {
        projectId: project.id,
        question: 'Which queue?',
        choice: 'pg-boss',
        rationale: 'one database',
        decidedBy: 'me',
        scope: 'project',
        area: null,
        sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'builder', {}),
      }),
    );
    const gone = recordDecision(project.id, 'Which queue runner?', { choice: 'cron' });
    store.write((ctx) => withdrawDecision(ctx, gone, 'me', 'mistake'));
    const pack = buildContextPack(store.db, workstream);
    expect(pack.knowledge.policies.map((p) => p.statement)).toEqual(['New a']);
    expect(pack.knowledge.decisions.map((d) => d.choice)).toEqual(['pg-boss']);
  });

  it('fills the budget and counts what each shelf dropped', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { title: 'Alpha badge' });
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) recordDecision(project.id, `Badge question ${String(i)}`);
    rule({ scope: 'project', projectId: project.id }, 'p', { statement: 'y'.repeat(100) });
    const pack = buildContextPack(store.db, workstream, 175);
    expect(pack.knowledge.policies).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(pack.knowledge.decisions.length + pack.knowledge.dropped.decisions).toBe(5);
    expect(pack.knowledge.dropped.decisions).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(pack.knowledge.droppedCount).toBe(pack.knowledge.dropped.decisions);
    expect(pack.knowledge.budgetUsed).toBeLessThanOrEqual(175);
    expect(pack.knowledge.empty).toBe(false);
  });

  it('falls back to recent decisions when nothing matches the task', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { title: 'Alpha' });
    recordDecision(project.id, 'Unrelated zebra topic', { choice: 'stripes' });
    const pack = buildContextPack(store.db, workstream);
    expect(pack.knowledge.decisions.map((d) => d.question)).toEqual(['Unrelated zebra topic']);
  });

  it('is deterministic for the same state', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { title: 'Alpha badge' });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) recordDecision(project.id, `Badge question ${String(i)}`);
    rule({ scope: 'project', projectId: project.id }, 'p');
    expect(buildContextPack(store.db, workstream, 150)).toEqual(
      buildContextPack(store.db, workstream, 150),
    );
  });

  it('still briefs from policies and the newest decisions when the decision search index is broken', () => {
    const { project, workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { title: 'Alpha queue' });
    rule({ scope: 'project', projectId: project.id }, 'p');
    recordDecision(project.id, 'Which queue?', { choice: 'pg-boss' });
    // Defensive mode keeps FTS5 shadow tables read-only; unsafe mode lets the test corrupt one, as an
    // index the startup check could not rebuild would be. Its MATCH now throws SQLITE_CORRUPT_VTAB.
    store.db.$client.unsafeMode(true);
    store.db.$client.exec('DROP TABLE decisions_fts_data; CREATE TABLE decisions_fts_data(x)');
    store.db.$client.unsafeMode(false);
    const pack = buildContextPack(store.db, workstream);
    expect(pack.knowledge.policies.map((p) => p.topicKey)).toEqual(['p']);
    expect(pack.knowledge.decisions.map((d) => d.choice)).toEqual(['pg-boss']);
  });
});
```

In `packages/contracts/src/mcp-tools.ts`, import `PrincipleId` and `KnowledgeScope`, `PrincipleKind`, and replace `KnowledgeHit` and `ContextPack.knowledge`:

```ts
export const KnowledgeHit = z.object({
  kind: z.literal('decision'),

});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Build the pack from the knowledge module**

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/areas.ts`:

```ts
import { ClaimKind, type WorkstreamDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { activeClaims, getTask, type DbReader } from '@10db/db';

/** The areas a workstream's knowledge is scoped to: its task's areas plus any area it has claimed since. */
export function workstreamAreas(
  db: DbReader,
  workstream: Pick<WorkstreamDto, 'id' | 'taskId'>,
): string[] {
  const claimed = activeClaims(db, workstream.id)
    .filter((c) => c.kind === ClaimKind.Area)
    .map((c) => c.name);
  return [...new Set([...getTask(db, workstream.taskId).areas, ...claimed])].sort();
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/hits.ts`:

```ts
import type { KnowledgeHit, PrincipleHit } from '@10db/contracts';
import type { PackDecision, PackPrinciple } from './pack';

export function decisionHit(d: PackDecision): KnowledgeHit {
  return {
    kind: 'decision',
    id: d.id,
    question: d.question,
    choice: d.choice,
    rationale: d.rationale,
    scope: d.scope,
    area: d.area,
  };
}

export function principleHit(p: PackPrinciple): PrincipleHit {
  return {
    kind: 'principle',
    id: p.id,
    statement: p.statement,
    principleKind: p.kind,
    topicKey: p.topicKey,
    scope: p.scope,
    area: p.area,
  };
}
```

Replace `apps/daemon/src/briefing/context-pack.ts`:

```ts
import {
  EventType,
  type ContextPack,
  type DecisionId,
  type ProjectId,
  type SessionId,
  type WorkstreamDto,
  type WorkstreamId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  activeClaims,
  getHandoff,
  getInboxItem,
  getTask,
  latestHandoffForStep,
  listDecisions,
  listPrinciples,
  searchDecisionIds,
  type DbReader,
  type EmitInput,
} from '@10db/db';
import {
  CONTEXT_PACK_BUDGET_TOKENS,
  decisionHit,
  PACK_SEARCH_LIMIT,
  packKnowledge,
  principleHit,
  workstreamAreas,
} from '../knowledge';

/**
 * The task's search hits among the decisions, best first. A decision index the startup check could
 * not rebuild throws here; it is already logged and flagged, so the pack falls back to the newest
 * decisions instead of failing the session's briefing.
 */
function rankedForTask(db: DbReader, projectId: ProjectId, query: string): DecisionId[] {
  try {
    return searchDecisionIds(db, { projectId, query, limit: PACK_SEARCH_LIMIT });
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Deterministic, budgeted context for a session (spec §9): the task, its live claims, the handoff to
 * continue from, any answer waiting to be delivered, and the knowledge that applies to the task.
 */
export function buildContextPack(
  db: DbReader,
  workstream: WorkstreamDto,
  budgetTokens: number = CONTEXT_PACK_BUDGET_TOKENS,
): ContextPack {
  const task = getTask(db, workstream.taskId);
  const action = workstream.nextAction;
  const handoff =
    action.kind === 'continue_from_handoff'
      ? getHandoff(db, action.handoffId)
      : latestHandoffForStep(db, workstream);
  const answered = action.kind === 'answer' ? getInboxItem(db, action.inboxId) : null;
  const { projectId } = workstream;
  const k = packKnowledge({
    areas: workstreamAreas(db, workstream),
    principles: listPrinciples(db, { projectId }),
    decisions: listDecisions(db, { projectId }),
    ranked: rankedForTask(db, projectId, `${task.title} ${task.description}`),
    budgetTokens,
  });
  const shown = k.policies.length + k.heuristics.length + k.decisions.length;
  return {
    task: {
      title: task.title,
      description: task.description,
      acceptanceCriteria: task.acceptanceCriteria,
    },
    claims: activeClaims(db, workstream.id).map((c) => ({ kind: c.kind, name: c.name })),
    handoff,
    pendingAnswer: answered?.answer
      ? {
          question: answered.title,
          answer: answered.answer.choice,
          note: answered.answer.note ?? null,
        }
      : null,
    knowledge: {
      policies: k.policies.map(principleHit),
      heuristics: k.heuristics.map(principleHit),
      decisions: k.decisions.map(decisionHit),
      budget: k.budgetTokens,
      budgetUsed: k.usedTokens,
      dropped: k.dropped,
      droppedCount: k.dropped.policies + k.dropped.heuristics + k.dropped.decisions,
      empty: shown === 0,
    },
  };
}

/** Why a pack was injected: a new conversation, a resumed one, or an emergency compaction (spec §9). */
export const PackReason = { Start: 'start', Resume: 'resume', Compaction: 'compaction' } as const;
export type PackReason = (typeof PackReason)[keyof typeof PackReason];

/** The event that records what an injected pack used and left out (spec §9). */
export function contextPackedEvent(
  sessionId: SessionId,
  workstreamId: WorkstreamId,
  pack: ContextPack,
  reason: PackReason,
): EmitInput {
  const k = pack.knowledge;
  return {
    type: EventType.ContextPacked,
    entityType: 'session',
    entityId: sessionId,
    data: {
      workstreamId,
      reason,
      budget: k.budget,
      budgetUsed: k.budgetUsed,
      dropped: { ...k.dropped },
      principles: [...k.policies, ...k.heuristics].map((p) => p.id),
      decisions: k.decisions.map((d) => d.id),
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Render the pack, and re-inject it as text**

In `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.ts`, replace `renderKnowledge` and `compactionReinjection`:

```ts
function renderKnowledge(pack: ContextPack): string {
  const k = pack.knowledge;
  const omitted =
    k.droppedCount > 0
      ? `${String(k.droppedCount)} records exceeded the budget (policies: ${String(k.dropped.policies)}, heuristics: ${String(k.dropped.heuristics)}, decisions: ${String(k.dropped.decisions)}); call find_knowledge for them.`
      : null;
  if (k.empty)
    return (
      omitted ??
      'No recorded decisions or principles apply to this task yet. Call find_knowledge if you need one.'
    );
  const lines: string[] = [];
  if (k.policies.length > 0)
    lines.push(
      'Policies (you must follow these):',
      bullets(k.policies.map((p) => `[${p.topicKey}] ${p.statement}`)),
    );
  if (k.heuristics.length > 0)
    lines.push(
      'Heuristics (prefer these):',
      bullets(k.heuristics.map((p) => `[${p.topicKey}] ${p.statement}`)),
    );
  if (k.decisions.length > 0)
    lines.push(
      'Recorded decisions:',
      bullets(k.decisions.map((d) => `${d.question} -> ${d.choice} (${d.rationale})`)),
    );
  if (omitted) lines.push(omitted);
  return lines.join('\n');
}
```

```ts
/** The pack again after an emergency compaction (spec §5.3), as the same text the session started with. */
export function compactionReinjection(pack: ContextPack): string {
  const claims = pack.claims.map((c) => `${c.kind} ${c.name}`).join(', ') || '(none)';
  return [
    '10db: your conversation was compacted. Here is your context pack again.',
    '',
    `Task: ${pack.task.title}`,
    pack.task.description,
    '',
    'Acceptance criteria:',
    bullets(pack.task.acceptanceCriteria),
    '',
    `Your claims: ${claims}.`,
    '',
    renderKnowledge(pack),
    ...(pack.handoff ? ['', 'Latest handoff:', renderHandoff(pack.handoff)] : []),
  ].join('\n');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write principle proposals**

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/proposals.ts`:

```ts
import {
  InboxKind,
  KnowledgeScope,
  PrincipleStatus,
  PrincipleVerdict,
  ProposalKind,
  QuestionCategory,
  topicKeyFrom,
  Urgency,
  type InboxItemDto,
  type InboxOption,
  type KnowledgeTarget,
  type PrincipleDraft,
  type PrincipleDto,
  type ProjectId,
  type ProposalId,
  type SourceId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { insertInboxItem, insertPrinciple, insertProposal, type WriteContext } from '@10db/db';

const SCOPE_WORDS: Readonly<Record<KnowledgeScope, string>> = {
  [KnowledgeScope.You]: 'you, in every project',
  [KnowledgeScope.Project]: 'this project',
  [KnowledgeScope.Area]: 'one area of this project',
};

/** The verdict that confirms a principle at its own scope; the card recommends it. */
const VERDICT_FOR_SCOPE: Readonly<Record<KnowledgeScope, PrincipleVerdict>> = {
  [KnowledgeScope.You]: PrincipleVerdict.AcceptYou,
  [KnowledgeScope.Project]: PrincipleVerdict.AcceptProject,
  [KnowledgeScope.Area]: PrincipleVerdict.AcceptArea,
};

/** The target a draft asks for, in the proposing project. */
function draftTarget(
  projectId: ProjectId,
  draft: Pick<PrincipleDraft, 'scope' | 'area'>,
): KnowledgeTarget {
  switch (draft.scope) {
    case KnowledgeScope.You:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.You };
    case KnowledgeScope.Project:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId };
    case KnowledgeScope.Area:
      // PrincipleDraft refuses an area scope without an area; the fallback only satisfies the type.
      return draft.area === undefined
        ? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId }
        : { scope: KnowledgeScope.Area, projectId, area: draft.area };
  }
}

/** A principle proposal card's options (spec §9 "with a choice of scope"); the area one only when it names one. */
function principleCardOptions(principle: Pick<PrincipleDto, 'area'>): InboxOption[] {
  return [
    { label: PrincipleVerdict.AcceptProject, description: 'Confirm it for this project' },
    { label: PrincipleVerdict.AcceptYou, description: 'Confirm it for you, in every project' },
    ...(principle.area === null
      ? []
      : [
          {
            label: PrincipleVerdict.AcceptArea,
            description: `Confirm it for the ${principle.area} area of this project`,
          },
        ]),
    { label: PrincipleVerdict.Reject, description: 'Reject it; it stays on record as withdrawn' },
  ];
}

/** Where a principle applies, in the card's words. */
function placeWords(p: Pick<PrincipleDto, 'scope' | 'area'>): string {
  return `${SCOPE_WORDS[p.scope]}${p.area === null ? '' : ` (${p.area})`}`;
}

// Agent text is data: the card shows it as plain text with where it came from (spec §7).
function cardBody(p: PrincipleDto, body: string): string {
  return [
    p.statement,
    '',
    `Kind: ${p.kind}. Key: ${p.topicKey}. Proposed for ${placeWords(p)}.`,
    ...(p.sourceQuote === null ? [] : [`Quote: "${p.sourceQuote}"`]),
    ...(body === p.statement ? [] : ['', body]),
  ].join('\n');
}

export interface PrincipleProposal {
  readonly projectId: ProjectId;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly body: string;
  readonly draft: PrincipleDraft;
  readonly sourceId: SourceId;
  /** Who stated it: `me`, or `agent:<session>`. */
  readonly speaker: string;
}

/** Stores a proposed principle, its pending proposal and the card that asks the builder (spec §9). */
export function proposePrinciple(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  input: PrincipleProposal,
): { principle: PrincipleDto; proposalId: ProposalId; card: InboxItemDto } {
  const principle = insertPrinciple(ctx, {
    target: draftTarget(input.projectId, input.draft),
    statement: input.draft.statement,
    kind: input.draft.kind,
    topicKey: input.draft.topicKey ?? topicKeyFrom(input.draft.statement),
    status: PrincipleStatus.Proposed,
    sourceId: input.sourceId,
    sourceQuote: input.draft.quote ?? null,
    speaker: input.speaker,
    confirmedBy: null,
  });
  const proposalId = insertProposal(ctx, {
    projectId: input.projectId,
    kind: ProposalKind.Principle,
    title: input.title,
    body: input.body,
    sourceId: input.sourceId,
    principleId: principle.id,
  });
  const card = insertInboxItem(ctx, {
    projectId: input.projectId,
    kind: InboxKind.Proposal,
    title: input.title,
    body: cardBody(principle, input.body),
    options: principleCardOptions(principle),
    recommendation: VERDICT_FOR_SCOPE[principle.scope],
    category: QuestionCategory.Product,
    urgency: Urgency.Low,
    detail: { proposalId, principleId: principle.id },
    foldKey: null,
    targets: [],
  });
  return { principle, proposalId, card };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the detector**

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/standing-rules.ts`:

```ts
/** At most this many rules are proposed from one answer, so a long note can't flood the inbox. */
export const MAX_STANDING_RULES = 3;
const MIN_WORDS = 3;
const STATEMENT_MAX = 500;

/** Sentences that state a standing rule with the word "always" or "never" (spec §9 capture). */
export function standingRules(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+|\n+/)
    .map((s) => s.trim().replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, ''))
    .filter((s) => /\b(?:always|never)\b/i.test(s) && s.split(/\s+/).length >= MIN_WORDS)
    .slice(0, MAX_STANDING_RULES)
    .map((s) => s.slice(0, STATEMENT_MAX));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write verdicts and capture**

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/targets.ts`:

```ts
import { InvalidError, KnowledgeScope, type KnowledgeTarget } from '@10db/contracts';
import { getProject, type DbReader } from '@10db/db';

/** A target's project must exist, and an area target must name one of its areas. */
export function checkTarget(db: DbReader, target: KnowledgeTarget): void {
  if (target.scope === KnowledgeScope.You) return;
  const project = getProject(db, target.projectId);
  if (target.scope === KnowledgeScope.Area && !project.areas.some((a) => a.name === target.area))
    throw new InvalidError(`${project.name} has no area named ${target.area}`);
}
```

Append to `apps/daemon/src/knowledge/proposals.ts` (add `ConflictError`, `InboxState`, `InvalidError`, `ProposalId`, `ProposalStatus`, `type InboxAnswer`, `type ProposalAccept`, `type ProposalDto` to the contracts import; `BUILDER_HANDLE`, `confirmPrinciple`, `findOpenProposalCard`, `getProposalDto`, `resolveProposal`, `updateInboxItem`, `withdrawPrinciple` to the `@10db/db` import; `z` from `zod`; and `checkTarget` from `./targets`):

```ts
/** A plain proposal card's options; a principle proposal's card uses PrincipleVerdict instead. */
const PlainChoice = { Accept: 'accept', Reject: 'reject' } as const;
const Verdict = z.enum(PrincipleVerdict);

/** The target a card verdict confirms at, or null for a rejection. */
function verdictTarget(
  verdict: PrincipleVerdict,
  principle: PrincipleDto,
  projectId: ProjectId,
): KnowledgeTarget | null {
  switch (verdict) {
    case PrincipleVerdict.AcceptProject:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId };
    case PrincipleVerdict.AcceptYou:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.You };
    case PrincipleVerdict.AcceptArea:
      if (principle.area === null)
        throw new InvalidError(
          'This proposal names no area; accept it for the project or for you, or choose an area in the Knowledge view.',
        );
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Area, projectId, area: principle.area };
    case PrincipleVerdict.Reject:
      return null;
  }
}

function storedTarget(p: PrincipleDto, projectId: ProjectId): KnowledgeTarget {
  return draftTarget(projectId, { scope: p.scope, ...(p.area === null ? {} : { area: p.area }) });
}

function pending(ctx: WriteContext, id: ProposalId): ProposalDto {
  const proposal = getProposalDto(ctx.tx, id);
  if (proposal.status !== ProposalStatus.Pending)
    throw new ConflictError(`Proposal ${id} is already ${proposal.status}`);
  return proposal;
}

/**
 * Accepts a proposal; its principle is confirmed at the accepted target, with any edits (spec §9).
 * The target is checked here, so the card and the Knowledge view refuse an unknown area alike.
 */
export function acceptProposal(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: ProposalId,
  accept: ProposalAccept,
  by: string,
): ProposalDto {
  const proposal = pending(ctx, id);
  const { principle } = proposal;
  if (principle) {
    const target = accept.target ?? storedTarget(principle, proposal.projectId);
    checkTarget(ctx.tx, target);
    confirmPrinciple(ctx, principle.id, {
      target,
      statement: accept.statement,
      kind: accept.kind,
      topicKey: accept.topicKey,
      confirmedBy: by,
    });
  } else if (
    [accept.target, accept.statement, accept.kind, accept.topicKey].some((v) => v !== undefined)
  ) {
    throw new InvalidError(
      'Only a principle proposal takes a target, statement, kind or topic key.',
    );
  }
  resolveProposal(ctx, id, { status: ProposalStatus.Accepted, rationale: accept.note ?? null });
  return getProposalDto(ctx.tx, id);
}

/** Rejects a proposal; a proposed principle is withdrawn with the note as its reason (spec §9 tombstones). */
export function rejectProposal(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: ProposalId,
  note: string | null,
  by: string,
): ProposalDto {
  const proposal = pending(ctx, id);
  if (proposal.principle) withdrawPrinciple(ctx, proposal.principle.id, by, note ?? 'Rejected');
  resolveProposal(ctx, id, { status: ProposalStatus.Rejected, rationale: note });
  return getProposalDto(ctx.tx, id);
}

/**
 * The card's answer for a verdict given in the Knowledge view. A principle accepted at a target the
 * card never offered (an area on a card without one, or another area than the one it named) records
 * a plain `accept` with the target in the note, so the card never shows a choice it didn't offer.
 */
function cardAnswer(
  card: InboxItemDto,
  proposal: ProposalDto,
  note: string | undefined,
): InboxAnswer {
  const answer = (choice: string, text: string | undefined): InboxAnswer =>
    text === undefined ? { choice } : { choice, note: text };
  if (proposal.status !== ProposalStatus.Accepted) return answer(PlainChoice.Reject, note);
  const { principle } = proposal;
  if (!principle) return answer(PlainChoice.Accept, note);
  // An area option's description names its area, so comparing it too tells one area from another.
  const verdict = principleCardOptions(principle).find(
    (o) => o.label === VERDICT_FOR_SCOPE[principle.scope],
  );
  if (
    verdict &&
    card.options.some((o) => o.label === verdict.label && o.description === verdict.description)
  )
    return answer(verdict.label, note);
  const where = `Accepted for ${placeWords(principle)}.`;
  return answer(PlainChoice.Accept, note === undefined ? where : `${where} ${note}`);
}

/** Resolves the proposal's open card after a verdict given in the Knowledge view. */
export function closeProposalCard(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  proposal: ProposalDto,
  note: string | undefined,
): void {
  const card = findOpenProposalCard(ctx.tx, proposal.id);
  if (!card) return;
  updateInboxItem(ctx, card.id, {
    state: InboxState.Resolved,
    answer: cardAnswer(card, proposal, note),
  });
}

/** A proposal card's answer, in the answer's own transaction: a principle verdict, or accept/reject. */
export function applyProposalCard(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  item: InboxItemDto,
  answer: InboxAnswer,
): void {
  const proposalId = ProposalId.safeParse(item.detail?.['proposalId']);
  if (!proposalId.success) return;
  const proposal = getProposalDto(ctx.tx, proposalId.data);
  const { note } = answer;
  if (proposal.principle) {
    // The inbox checked the choice against the card's options, which are verdicts.
    const target = verdictTarget(
      Verdict.parse(answer.choice),
      proposal.principle,
      proposal.projectId,
    );
    if (target === null) rejectProposal(ctx, proposal.id, note ?? null, BUILDER_HANDLE);
    else
      acceptProposal(
        ctx,
        proposal.id,
        note === undefined ? { target } : { target, note },
        BUILDER_HANDLE,
      );
    return;
  }
  if (answer.choice === PlainChoice.Accept)
    acceptProposal(ctx, proposal.id, note === undefined ? {} : { note }, BUILDER_HANDLE);
  else rejectProposal(ctx, proposal.id, note ?? null, BUILDER_HANDLE);
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/capture.ts`:

```ts
import {
  DECISION_CATEGORIES,
  InboxKind,
  InvalidError,
  KnowledgeScope,
  PrincipleKind,
  SourceKind,
  targetColumns,
  topicKeyFrom,
  type DecisionId,
  type DecisionRecording,
  type InboxAnswer,
  type InboxItemDto,
  type KnowledgeTarget,
  type ProjectDto,
  type ProjectId,
  type ProposalId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { BUILDER_HANDLE, insertDecision, insertSource, type WriteContext } from '@10db/db';
import { proposePrinciple } from './proposals';
import { standingRules } from './standing-rules';

const PROJECT_RECORDING: DecisionRecording = { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, area: null };

function recordingTarget(r: DecisionRecording, projectId: ProjectId): KnowledgeTarget {
  switch (r.scope) {
    case KnowledgeScope.You:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.You };
    case KnowledgeScope.Project:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId };
    case KnowledgeScope.Area:
      // DecisionRecording refuses an area scope without an area; the fallback only satisfies the type.
      return r.area === null
        ? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId }
        : { scope: KnowledgeScope.Area, projectId, area: r.area };
  }
}

/** Whether and where an answered question becomes a decision (spec §9 "Inbox answers become decisions"). */
export function answerRecording(item: InboxItemDto, answer: InboxAnswer): DecisionRecording | null {
  if (item.kind !== InboxKind.Question) return null;
  if (answer.record !== undefined) return answer.record;
  return DECISION_CATEGORIES.includes(item.category) ? PROJECT_RECORDING : null;
}

/** Records the answer as a decision where it says (or its category implies), with the card as its source. */
export function recordAnswerDecision(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  item: InboxItemDto,
  answer: InboxAnswer,
  project: ProjectDto,
): DecisionId | null {
  const recording = answerRecording(item, answer);
  if (recording === null) return null;
  if (recording.area !== null && !project.areas.some((a) => a.name === recording.area))
    throw new InvalidError(`${project.name} has no area named ${recording.area}`);
  const sourceId = insertSource(ctx, SourceKind.InboxAnswer, { inboxId: item.id });
  return insertDecision(ctx, {
    ...targetColumns(recordingTarget(recording, item.projectId)),
    question: item.title,
    choice: answer.choice,
    rationale: answer.note ?? 'Answered in the inbox',
    decidedBy: BUILDER_HANDLE,
    sourceId,
  });
}

/** Each always/never sentence in an answer's note becomes a principle proposal for the builder (spec §9). */
export function proposeStandingRules(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  item: InboxItemDto,
  answer: InboxAnswer,
): ProposalId[] {
  if (item.kind !== InboxKind.Question || answer.note === undefined) return [];
  const rules = standingRules(answer.note);
  if (rules.length === 0) return [];
  const sourceId = insertSource(ctx, SourceKind.InboxAnswer, { inboxId: item.id });
  return rules.map(
    (statement) =>
      proposePrinciple(ctx, {
        projectId: item.projectId,
        title: `Make this a principle? ${statement}`,
        body: `You wrote this answering "${item.title}".`,
        draft: {
          statement,
          kind: PrincipleKind.Policy,
          topicKey: topicKeyFrom(statement),
          scope: KnowledgeScope.Project,
          quote: statement,
        },
        sourceId,
        speaker: BUILDER_HANDLE,
      }).proposalId,
  );
}
```

```ts
const rule = (projectId: ReturnType<typeof seedProject>['id'], topicKey = 'testing.no-sleeps') =>

describe('the override review', () => {
  it.each([
    { name: 'one override', days: [0], cards: 0 },
    { name: 'two within 30 days', days: [0, 29], cards: 1 },
    { name: 'three within 30 days, folded into one card', days: [0, 10, 20], cards: 1 },
    { name: 'two 31 days apart', days: [0, 31], cards: 0 },

          severity: 'warn',
          signedReason: `upstream was flaky on day ${String(day)} (agent:x)`,
          sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'agent', {}),

        });

      });

    const open = listInbox(store.db, { states: ['OPEN'] });
    expect(open).toHaveLength(cards);
    if (cards === 1) {
      expect(open[0]?.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(['keep', 'retire']);
      expect(open[0]?.body).toContain('upstream was flaky');
      expect(open[0]?.detail).toMatchObject({ principleReview: principle.id });
    }
  });

  it('never counts another principle’s overrides', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const a = rule(project.id, 'a');
    const b = rule(project.id, 'b');
    for (const principle of [a, b])
      store.write((ctx) => {
        insertDeviation(ctx, {
          projectId: project.id,
          workstreamId: null,
          cause: 'c',
          severity: 'warn',

  it('resolves a key to the most specific current principle for the areas', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const broad = rule(project.id);
    const narrow = store.write((ctx) =>
      insertPrinciple(ctx, {
        target: { scope: 'area', projectId: project.id, area: 'api' },
        statement: 'Never sleep in api tests',
        kind: 'policy',
        topicKey: 'testing.no-sleeps',

        speaker: 'me',

    expect(principleForKey(store.db, project.id, ['ui'], 'testing.no-sleeps')?.id).toBe(broad.id);
    expect(principleForKey(store.db, project.id, ['api'], 'unknown')).toBeUndefined();
    // A belief is never packed, so a ui-area belief with the key doesn't hide the project policy.
    store.write((ctx) =>

        confirmedBy: 'me',
      }),
    );
    expect(principleForKey(store.db, project.id, ['ui'], 'testing.no-sleeps')?.id).toBe(broad.id);
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/override-review.ts`:

```ts
import {
  InboxKind,
  KnowledgeState,
  PRINCIPLE_REVIEW_DETAIL,
  PrincipleId,
  PrincipleKind,
  PrincipleReviewChoice,
  QuestionCategory,
  Urgency,
  type InboxAnswer,
  type InboxItemDto,
  type PrincipleDto,
  type ProjectId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  BUILDER_HANDLE,
  findOpenByFoldKey,
  getPrinciple,
  insertInboxItem,
  listPrinciples,
  recentDeviationsFor,
  retirePrinciple,
  type DbReader,
  type WriteContext,
} from '@10db/db';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { mostSpecific } from './scope';

/** Spec §9: two recent signed overrides of a principle raise a "review this principle?" card. */
export const OVERRIDE_REVIEW_COUNT = 2;
export const OVERRIDE_REVIEW_WINDOW_DAYS = 30;
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const Choice = z.enum(PrincipleReviewChoice);

/**
 * The most specific current policy or heuristic with this key that applies to work in `areas`: the
 * rule the pack showed under that key. Beliefs are never packed, so they are dropped first, as the
 * packer drops them.
 */
export function principleForKey(
  db: DbReader,
  projectId: ProjectId,
  areas: readonly string[],
  key: string,
): PrincipleDto | undefined {
  return mostSpecific(
    listPrinciples(db, { projectId }).filter(
      (p) => p.topicKey === key && p.kind !== PrincipleKind.Belief,
    ),
    areas,
  )[0];
}

/** Raises one folded review card once a principle has enough overrides inside the window. */
export function noteOverride(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  principle: PrincipleDto,
  projectId: ProjectId,
): InboxItemDto | null {
  const since = new Date(Date.parse(ctx.now) - OVERRIDE_REVIEW_WINDOW_DAYS * DAY_MS).toISOString();
  const overrides = recentDeviationsFor(ctx.tx, principle.id, since);
  if (overrides.length < OVERRIDE_REVIEW_COUNT) return null;
  const foldKey = `principle-review:${principle.id}`;
  const open = findOpenByFoldKey(ctx.tx, projectId, foldKey);
  if (open) return open;
  return insertInboxItem(ctx, {
    projectId,
    kind: InboxKind.Flag,
    title: `Review this principle? ${principle.statement}`,
    // The reasons are agent text, shown as data.
    body: [
      `Agents departed from [${principle.topicKey}] ${String(overrides.length)} times in the last ${String(OVERRIDE_REVIEW_WINDOW_DAYS)} days:`,
      ...overrides.map((o) => `- ${o.signedReason}`),
    ].join('\n'),
    options: [
      { label: PrincipleReviewChoice.Keep, description: 'Keep it as it is' },
      { label: PrincipleReviewChoice.Retire, description: 'Retire it; sessions stop seeing it' },
    ],
    recommendation: null,
    category: QuestionCategory.Engineering,
    urgency: Urgency.Normal,
    detail: { [PRINCIPLE_REVIEW_DETAIL]: principle.id, deviationIds: overrides.map((o) => o.id) },
    foldKey,
    targets: [],
  });
}

/** A review card's answer, in the answer's transaction: `retire` retires the principle if it is still current. */
export function applyPrincipleReview(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  item: InboxItemDto,
  answer: InboxAnswer,
): void {
  const id = PrincipleId.safeParse(item.detail?.[PRINCIPLE_REVIEW_DETAIL]);
  if (!id.success) return;
  switch (Choice.parse(answer.choice)) {
    case PrincipleReviewChoice.Keep:
      return;
    case PrincipleReviewChoice.Retire:
      if (getPrinciple(ctx.tx, id.data).state === KnowledgeState.Current)
        retirePrinciple(
          ctx,
          id.data,
          BUILDER_HANDLE,
          answer.note ?? 'Retired after a review of repeated overrides',
        );
      return;
  }
}
```
