  - `knowledge/records.ts`: `LIST_SEARCH_LIMIT = 100`, `decisionsFor(db, q: KnowledgeQuery): DecisionDto[]`, `principlesFor(db, q: KnowledgeQuery): PrincipleDto[]`, `recordDecision(ctx, input: DecisionInput, supersedes?: DecisionId): DecisionDto`, `createPrinciple(ctx, input: PrincipleInput): PrincipleDto`.
  - `knowledge/provenance.ts`: `decisionProvenance(db, id: DecisionId): ProvenanceDto`, `principleProvenance(db, id: PrincipleId): ProvenanceDto`.
  - `knowledge/index-health.ts`: `KnowledgeIndexRepair` (`{ rebuilt: KnowledgeIndex[]; failed: KnowledgeIndex[] }`), `repairKnowledgeIndex(db: TendbDatabase, log: Logger): KnowledgeIndexRepair` (never throws), `brokenIndexFlag(index: KnowledgeIndex): { title; body; foldKey }`.
  - `daemon/start.ts`: after the inbox exists, each project gets one folded flag card per index that failed.
  - `@10db/db`: `getSource(db, id: SourceId): { id; kind: SourceKind; ref: Record<string, Json>; createdAt: string }`.
  - `ApiServices`: `listDecisions(q: KnowledgeQuery)`, `recordDecision(input: DecisionInput)`, `supersedeDecision(id, input: DecisionInput)`, `withdrawDecision(id, input: ReasonInput)`, `decisionProvenance(id)`, `listPrinciples(q: KnowledgeQuery)`, `createPrinciple(input: PrincipleInput)`, `retirePrinciple(id, input: ReasonInput)`, `withdrawPrinciple(id, input: ReasonInput)`, `principleProvenance(id)`, `listProposals(q: ProposalQuery)`, `acceptProposal(id, input: ProposalAccept)`, `rejectProposal(id, input: ProposalReject)`.
  - Routes (the UI's client types come from these):

    | Method and path                  | Body or query                                              | Answer                            |
    | -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |
    | `GET /decisions`                 | `projectId`, `scope?`, `area?`, `q?`, `history?` (`0`/`1`) | `DecisionDto[]`                   |
    | `POST /decisions`                | `DecisionInput`                                            | 201 `DecisionDto`                 |
    | `POST /decisions/:id/supersede`  | `DecisionInput`                                            | 201 `DecisionDto` (the successor) |
    | `POST /decisions/:id/withdraw`   | `ReasonInput`                                              | `DecisionDto`                     |
    | `GET /decisions/:id/provenance`  |                                                            | `ProvenanceDto`                   |
    | `GET /principles`                | as decisions, plus `kind?`                                 | `PrincipleDto[]`                  |
    | `POST /principles`               | `PrincipleInput`                                           | 201 `PrincipleDto`                |
    | `POST /principles/:id/retire`    | `ReasonInput`                                              | `PrincipleDto`                    |
    | `POST /principles/:id/withdraw`  | `ReasonInput`                                              | `PrincipleDto`                    |
    | `GET /principles/:id/provenance` |                                                            | `ProvenanceDto`                   |
    | `GET /proposals`                 | `projectId`, `status?` (default `pending`)                 | `ProposalDto[]`                   |
    | `POST /proposals/:id/accept`     | `ProposalAccept`                                           | `ProposalDto`                     |
    | `POST /proposals/:id/reject`     | `ProposalReject`                                           | `ProposalDto`                     |

    A list with `q` returns search hits best first (history included when `history=1`); without `q`, newest first. An unknown project is 404; an area the project lacks (in a record, a principle or an accepted proposal's target), a bad query string or body is 400; a second verdict, superseding or withdrawing a decision that isn't current, retiring a principle that isn't current and withdrawing one that is neither current nor proposed are 409.

```ts
import { mintId, type KnowledgeQuery, type ProjectDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { createTestStore, insertSource, seedProject, type Store } from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { proposePrinciple } from './proposals';
import { decisionProvenance, principleProvenance } from './provenance';
import { createPrinciple, decisionsFor, principlesFor, recordDecision } from './records';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let project: ProjectDto;
beforeEach(() => {
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore());
  project = seedProject(store);
});
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

const query = (over: Partial<KnowledgeQuery> = {}): KnowledgeQuery => ({
  projectId: project.id,
  history: false,
  ...over,
});
const decide = (question: string, choice: string) =>
  store.write((ctx) =>
    recordDecision(ctx, {
      target: { scope: 'project', projectId: project.id },
      question,
      choice,
      rationale: 'r',
    }),
  ).value;

describe('knowledge records', () => {
  it('records a decision from the UI and reads it back through the list search', () => {
    const d = decide('Which queue library?', 'BullMQ');
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ state: 'current', decidedBy: 'me' });
    expect(decisionsFor(store.db, query({ q: 'queue' })).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual([d.id]);
    expect(decisionsFor(store.db, query()).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual([d.id]);
  });

  it('supersedes through recordDecision and searches history with q', () => {
    const old = decide('Which queue library?', 'BullMQ');
    const next = store.write((ctx) =>
      recordDecision(
        ctx,
        {
          target: { scope: 'you' },
          question: 'Which queue library?',
          choice: 'pg-boss',
          rationale: 'one db',
        },
        old.id,
      ),
    ).value;
    expect(decisionsFor(store.db, query({ q: 'queue' })).map((x) => x.choice)).toEqual(['pg-boss']);
    expect(
      decisionsFor(store.db, query({ q: 'queue', history: true })).map((x) => [x.choice, x.state]),
    ).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        ['BullMQ', 'superseded'],
        ['pg-boss', 'current'],
      ]),
    );
    expect(next.supersedesId).toBe(old.id);
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'an area the project lacks',
      target: () => ({ scope: 'area' as const, projectId: project.id, area: 'billing' }),
      error: /no area named billing/,
    },
    {
      name: 'an unknown project',
      target: () => ({ scope: 'project' as const, projectId: mintId('Project') }),
      error: /not found/,
    },
  ])('refuses a decision for $name', ({ target, error }) => {
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) =>
        recordDecision(ctx, { target: target(), question: 'q', choice: 'c', rationale: 'r' }),
      ),
    ).toThrow(error);
  });

  it('creates a confirmed principle that supersedes the one with its key, found by a kind-filtered search', () => {
    const input = {
      target: { scope: 'project' as const, projectId: project.id },
      statement: 'Never sleep in tests',
      kind: 'policy' as const,
      topicKey: 'testing.no-sleeps',
    };
    const first = store.write((ctx) => createPrinciple(ctx, input)).value;
    const second = store.write((ctx) =>
      createPrinciple(ctx, { ...input, statement: 'Never sleep or poll in tests' }),
    ).value;
    expect(second.supersedesId).toBe(first.id);
    expect(principlesFor(store.db, query({ q: 'sleep', kind: 'policy' })).map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(
      [second.id],
    );
    expect(principlesFor(store.db, query({ q: 'sleep', kind: 'heuristic' }))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('traces a decision to the builder, an answer to its card, and an agent principle to its session', () => {
    const d = decide('Which queue library?', 'BullMQ');
    expect(decisionProvenance(store.db, d.id)).toMatchObject({
      sourceKind: 'builder',
      by: 'me',
      inbox: null,
      session: null,
    });
    const { principle } = store.write((ctx) =>
      proposePrinciple(ctx, {
        projectId: project.id,
        title: 't',
        body: 'b',
        draft: {
          statement: 'Never sleep in tests',
          kind: 'policy',
          scope: 'project',
          quote: 'flaky',
        },
        sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'agent', {}),
        speaker: 'agent:ses_x',
      }),
    ).value;
    expect(principleProvenance(store.db, principle.id)).toMatchObject({
      sourceKind: 'agent',
      by: 'agent:ses_x',
      quote: 'flaky',
      confirmedBy: null,
    });
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/index-health.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  createTestStore,
  insertDecision,
  insertSource,
  searchDecisions,
  seedProject,
  type Store,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Logger } from '../log';
import { repairKnowledgeIndex } from './index-health';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
beforeEach(() => ({ store, close } = createTestStore()));
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

function recordingLogger() {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const logger: Logger = {
    debug: () => undefined,
    info: () => undefined,
    warn: () => undefined,
    error: (_fields, message) => {
      errors.push(message ?? '');
    },
    child: () => logger,
  };
  return { logger, errors };
}

const decide = (projectId: ReturnType<typeof seedProject>['id']) =>
  store.write((ctx) =>
    insertDecision(ctx, {
      projectId,
      question: 'Which queue library?',
      choice: 'BullMQ',
      rationale: 'Redis is already deployed',
      decidedBy: 'me',
      scope: 'project',
      area: null,
      sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'builder', {}),
    }),
  );

describe('repairKnowledgeIndex', () => {
  it('rebuilds an index out of step with its table, logs it, and search finds the record again', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    store.db.$client.exec('DROP TRIGGER decisions_fts_insert');
    decide(project.id);
    const { logger, errors } = recordingLogger();
    expect(repairKnowledgeIndex(store.db, logger)).toEqual({
      rebuilt: ['decisions_fts'],
      failed: [],
    });
    expect(errors).toEqual(['knowledge search index disagreed with its table; rebuilding it']);
    expect(searchDecisions(store.db, project.id, 'queue', 5).map((d) => d.choice)).toEqual([
      'BullMQ',
    ]);
  });

  it('logs a rebuild that fails on a corrupt shadow table and returns it, without throwing', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    decide(project.id);
    // SQLite's defensive mode keeps FTS5 shadow tables read-only; unsafe mode lets the test corrupt one.
    // A data table of the wrong shape fails the integrity check with SQLITE_CORRUPT_VTAB and the rebuild
    // with SQLITE_ERROR.
    store.db.$client.unsafeMode(true);
    store.db.$client.exec('DROP TABLE decisions_fts_data; CREATE TABLE decisions_fts_data(x)');
    store.db.$client.unsafeMode(false);
    const { logger, errors } = recordingLogger();
    expect(repairKnowledgeIndex(store.db, logger)).toEqual({
      rebuilt: [],
      failed: ['decisions_fts'],
    });
    expect(errors).toEqual([
      'knowledge search index disagreed with its table; rebuilding it',
      'rebuilding the knowledge search index failed; search over it fails until it is repaired',
    ]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { DecisionDto, PrincipleDto, ProjectDto, ProposalDto, ProvenanceDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { insertSource } from '@10db/db';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { apiClient } from '../../test/api-client';
import { createTempRepo, tempDir } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { proposePrinciple } from '../knowledge';
import { FakeAdapter } from '../sessions';
import { startDaemon, type RunningDaemon } from './index';

let dir: ReturnType<typeof tempDir>;
let repo: ReturnType<typeof createTempRepo>;
let daemon: RunningDaemon | null = null;
beforeEach(() => {
  dir = tempDir('knowledge');
  repo = createTempRepo();
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await daemon?.stop();
  daemon = null;
  repo.cleanup();
  dir.cleanup();
});

const launch = () =>
  startDaemon({
    dataDir: dir.path,
    port: 0,
    adapter: new FakeAdapter({}),
    logLevel: 'warn',
    caffeinate: () => null,
    watch: false,
  });

async function start() {
  daemon = await launch();
  const api = apiClient(daemon.url, daemon.token);
  const project = await api.post('/projects', ProjectDto, {
    name: 'demo',
    repoPath: repo.path,
    gateCommand: 'true',
    areas: [{ name: 'api', globs: ['src/api/**'] }],
  });
  return { api, project, state: daemon.state };
}

const Decisions = z.array(DecisionDto);

describe('knowledge over HTTP', () => {
  it('records, searches, supersedes and withdraws a decision, and shows where it came from', async () => {
    const { api, project } = await start();
    const target = { scope: 'project', projectId: project.id };
    const first = await api.post('/decisions', DecisionDto, {
      target,
      question: 'Which queue library?',
      choice: 'BullMQ',
      rationale: 'Redis is there',
    });
    expect(await api.get(`/decisions?projectId=${project.id}&q=queue`, Decisions)).toEqual([first]);
    const next = await api.post(`/decisions/${first.id}/supersede`, DecisionDto, {
      target,
      question: 'Which queue library?',
      choice: 'pg-boss',
      rationale: 'one database',
    });
    expect(
      (await api.get(`/decisions?projectId=${project.id}&q=queue`, Decisions)).map((d) => d.choice),
    ).toEqual(['pg-boss']);
    expect(
      (await api.get(`/decisions?projectId=${project.id}&q=queue&history=1`, Decisions))
        .map((d) => d.state)
        .sort(),
    ).toEqual(['current', 'superseded']);
    await expect(
      api.post(`/decisions/${first.id}/supersede`, DecisionDto, {
        target,
        question: 'q',
        choice: 'c',
        rationale: 'r',
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow('-> 409');
    await api.post(`/decisions/${next.id}/withdraw`, DecisionDto, { reason: 'reverted' });
    expect(
      (await api.get(`/decisions?projectId=${project.id}&q=queue`, Decisions)).map((d) => d.choice),
    ).toEqual(['BullMQ']);
    expect(await api.get(`/decisions/${first.id}/provenance`, ProvenanceDto)).toMatchObject({
      sourceKind: 'builder',
      by: 'me',
    });
  });

  it('creates, filters and retires principles, and refuses an area the project lacks', async () => {
    const { api, project } = await start();
    const created = await api.post('/principles', PrincipleDto, {
      target: { scope: 'area', projectId: project.id, area: 'api' },
      statement: 'Validate every request body with zod',
      kind: 'policy',
      topicKey: 'api.validate',
    });
    expect(
      (
        await api.get(
          `/principles?projectId=${project.id}&q=zod&scope=area&area=api`,
          z.array(PrincipleDto),
        )
      ).map((p) => p.id),
    ).toEqual([created.id]);
    await api.post(`/principles/${created.id}/retire`, PrincipleDto, {
      reason: 'the framework does it',
    });
    expect(
      await api.get(`/principles?projectId=${project.id}&q=zod`, z.array(PrincipleDto)),
    ).toEqual([]);
    await expect(
      api.post('/principles', PrincipleDto, {
        target: { scope: 'area', projectId: project.id, area: 'billing' },
        statement: 's',
        kind: 'policy',
        topicKey: 'k',
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow('-> 400');
  });

  it('accepts a proposal with edits, closes its card, and answers 409 to a second accept', async () => {
    const { api, project, state } = await start();
    const { proposalId } = state.write((ctx) =>
      proposePrinciple(ctx, {
        projectId: project.id,
        title: 'No sleeps',
        body: 'b',
        draft: { statement: 'Never sleep in tests', kind: 'policy', scope: 'project' },
        sourceId: insertSource(ctx, 'agent', {}),
        speaker: 'agent:x',
      }),
    );
    expect(
      (await api.get(`/proposals?projectId=${project.id}`, z.array(ProposalDto))).map((p) => p.id),
    ).toEqual([proposalId]);
    const accepted = await api.post(`/proposals/${proposalId}/accept`, ProposalDto, {
      target: { scope: 'you' },
      topicKey: 'testing.no-sleeps',
      note: 'everywhere',
    });
    expect(accepted).toMatchObject({
      status: 'accepted',
      principle: { state: 'current', scope: 'you' },
    });
    expect(await api.get(`/proposals?projectId=${project.id}`, z.array(ProposalDto))).toEqual([]);
    expect(
      (await api.get(`/principles?projectId=${project.id}&q=sleep`, z.array(PrincipleDto))).map(
        (p) => p.topicKey,
      ),
    ).toEqual(['testing.no-sleeps']);
    const cards = await api.get('/inbox?state=OPEN', z.array(z.object({ kind: z.string() })));
    expect(cards.filter((c) => c.kind === 'proposal')).toEqual([]);
    await expect(api.post(`/proposals/${proposalId}/accept`, ProposalDto, {})).rejects.toThrow(
      '-> 409',
    );
  });

  it('keeps running when a search index cannot be rebuilt at start, and flags it in each project', async () => {
    const { project } = await start();
    await daemon?.stop();
    daemon = null;
    // Defensive mode keeps FTS5 shadow tables read-only; unsafe mode lets the test corrupt one so the
    // next start's rebuild fails.
    const raw = new Database(join(dir.path, '10db.sqlite'));
    raw.unsafeMode(true);
    raw.exec('DROP TABLE decisions_fts_data; CREATE TABLE decisions_fts_data(x)');
    raw.close();
    daemon = await launch();
    const api = apiClient(daemon.url, daemon.token);
    const Card = z.object({ projectId: z.string(), kind: z.string(), title: z.string() });
    expect(await api.get('/inbox?state=OPEN', z.array(Card))).toEqual([
      { projectId: project.id, kind: 'flag', title: 'Knowledge search is broken' },
    ]);
    expect(await api.get(`/principles?projectId=${project.id}`, z.array(PrincipleDto))).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
export function getSource(db: DbReader, id: SourceId): typeof schema.sources.$inferSelect {
  const row = db.select().from(schema.sources).where(eq(schema.sources.id, id)).get();
  if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('source', id);
  return row;
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/records.ts`:

```ts
import {
  PrincipleStatus,
  SourceKind,
  targetColumns,
  type DecisionDto,
  type DecisionId,
  type DecisionInput,
  type KnowledgeQuery,
  type PrincipleDto,
  type PrincipleInput,
  type SourceId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  BUILDER_HANDLE,
  getDecision,
  getInboxItem,
  getProject,
  insertDecision,
  insertPrinciple,
  insertSource,
  listDecisions,
  listPrinciples,
  searchDecisionIds,
  searchPrincipleIds,
  supersedeDecision,
  type DbReader,
  type WriteContext,
} from '@10db/db';
import { checkTarget } from './targets';

/** How many search hits a list route returns. */
export const LIST_SEARCH_LIMIT = 100;

function narrowing(q: KnowledgeQuery) {
  return {
    projectId: q.projectId,
    ...(q.scope ? { scope: q.scope } : {}),
    ...(q.area ? { area: q.area } : {}),
  };
}

/** The list route: search hits best first when there is a query, else newest first. */
export function decisionsFor(db: DbReader, q: KnowledgeQuery): DecisionDto[] {
  getProject(db, q.projectId);
  const base = narrowing(q);
  if (!q.q) return listDecisions(db, { ...base, history: q.history });
  const ids = searchDecisionIds(db, {
    ...base,
    query: q.q,
    limit: LIST_SEARCH_LIMIT,
    history: q.history,
  });
  return listDecisions(db, { ...base, ids, history: true });
}

export function principlesFor(db: DbReader, q: KnowledgeQuery): PrincipleDto[] {
  getProject(db, q.projectId);
  const base = { ...narrowing(q), ...(q.kind ? { kind: q.kind } : {}) };
  if (!q.q) return listPrinciples(db, { ...base, history: q.history });
  const ids = searchPrincipleIds(db, {
    ...base,
    query: q.q,
    limit: LIST_SEARCH_LIMIT,
    history: q.history,
  });
  return listPrinciples(db, { ...base, ids, history: true });
}

function sourceFor(ctx: WriteContext, input: DecisionInput): SourceId {
  if (input.fromInboxId === undefined) return insertSource(ctx, SourceKind.Builder, {});
  getInboxItem(ctx.tx, input.fromInboxId);
  return insertSource(ctx, SourceKind.InboxAnswer, { inboxId: input.fromInboxId });
}

/** The builder records a decision, or supersedes one, from the Knowledge view. */
export function recordDecision(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  input: DecisionInput,
  supersedes?: DecisionId,
): DecisionDto {
  checkTarget(ctx.tx, input.target);
  const decision = {
    ...targetColumns(input.target),
    question: input.question,
    choice: input.choice,
    rationale: input.rationale,
    decidedBy: BUILDER_HANDLE,
    sourceId: sourceFor(ctx, input),
  };
  const id =
    supersedes === undefined
      ? insertDecision(ctx, decision)
      : supersedeDecision(ctx, supersedes, decision);
  return getDecision(ctx.tx, id);
}

/** The builder writes a principle; it is confirmed at once and supersedes the one with its key and target. */
export function createPrinciple(ctx: WriteContext, input: PrincipleInput): PrincipleDto {
  checkTarget(ctx.tx, input.target);
  return insertPrinciple(ctx, {
    target: input.target,
    statement: input.statement,
    kind: input.kind,
    topicKey: input.topicKey,
    status: PrincipleStatus.Confirmed,
    sourceId: insertSource(ctx, SourceKind.Builder, {}),
    sourceQuote: input.quote ?? null,
    speaker: BUILDER_HANDLE,
    confirmedBy: BUILDER_HANDLE,
  });
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/provenance.ts`:

```ts
import {
  InboxItemId,
  SessionId,
  WorkstreamId,
  type DecisionId,
  type PrincipleId,
  type ProvenanceDto,
  type SourceId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  BUILDER_HANDLE,
  getDecision,
  getInboxItem,
  getPrinciple,
  getSession,
  getSource,
  getWorkstream,
  type DbReader,
} from '@10db/db';
import { z } from 'zod';

// A source's ref is JSON 10db wrote; it is parsed again because it is read back from disk.
const SourceRef = z.object({
  inboxId: InboxItemId.optional(),
  sessionId: SessionId.optional(),
  workstreamId: WorkstreamId.optional(),
});

interface Attribution {
  readonly sourceId: SourceId;
  readonly by: string;
  readonly quote: string | null;
  readonly speaker: string | null;
  readonly confirmedBy: string | null;
  readonly confirmedAt: string | null;
}

function provenanceOf(db: DbReader, a: Attribution): ProvenanceDto {
  const source = getSource(db, a.sourceId);
  const ref = SourceRef.parse(source.ref);
  const card = ref.inboxId ? getInboxItem(db, ref.inboxId) : null;
  const workstreamId =
    ref.workstreamId ?? (ref.sessionId ? getSession(db, ref.sessionId).workstreamId : null);
  return {
    sourceKind: source.kind,
    recordedAt: source.createdAt,
    by: a.by,
    inbox: card
      ? {
          id: card.id,
          title: card.title,
          choice: card.answer?.choice ?? null,
          note: card.answer?.note ?? null,
        }
      : null,
    session:
      ref.sessionId && workstreamId
        ? { id: ref.sessionId, workstreamId, taskTitle: getWorkstream(db, workstreamId).taskTitle }
        : null,
    quote: a.quote,
    speaker: a.speaker,
    confirmedBy: a.confirmedBy,
    confirmedAt: a.confirmedAt,
  };
}

export function decisionProvenance(db: DbReader, id: DecisionId): ProvenanceDto {
  const d = getDecision(db, id);
  return provenanceOf(db, {
    sourceId: d.sourceId,
    by: d.decidedBy,
    quote: null,
    speaker: null,
    confirmedBy: null,
    confirmedAt: null,
  });
}

export function principleProvenance(db: DbReader, id: PrincipleId): ProvenanceDto {
  const p = getPrinciple(db, id);
  return provenanceOf(db, {
    sourceId: p.sourceId,
    by: p.speaker ?? p.confirmedBy ?? BUILDER_HANDLE,
    quote: p.sourceQuote,
    speaker: p.speaker,
    confirmedBy: p.confirmedBy,
    confirmedAt: p.confirmedAt,
  });
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/knowledge/index-health.ts`:

```ts
import {
  checkKnowledgeIndex,
  KnowledgeIndex,
  rebuildKnowledgeIndex,
  type TendbDatabase,
} from '@10db/db';
import type { Logger } from '../log';

export interface KnowledgeIndexRepair {
  /** Indexes that disagreed with their tables and were rebuilt. */
  readonly rebuilt: readonly KnowledgeIndex[];
  /** Indexes that could not be rebuilt; search over them fails until they are repaired. */
  readonly failed: readonly KnowledgeIndex[];
}

/**
 * The runtime half of spec §12's knowledge check: an index out of step with its table is rebuilt at
 * start. A check or rebuild that fails is logged and returned, never thrown, so a broken index can't
 * stop the daemon (spec §12: a parse failure becomes a flagged event, never a daemon crash).
 */
export function repairKnowledgeIndex(db: TendbDatabase, log: Logger): KnowledgeIndexRepair {
  let broken: readonly KnowledgeIndex[];
  try {
    broken = checkKnowledgeIndex(db);
  } catch (error) {
    // The check itself failed for a reason other than corruption; a rebuild is the only repair to try.
    log.error({ err: error }, 'checking the knowledge search indexes failed; rebuilding both');
    broken = Object.values(KnowledgeIndex);
  }
  const rebuilt: KnowledgeIndex[] = [];
  const failed: KnowledgeIndex[] = [];
  for (const index of broken) {
    log.error({ index }, 'knowledge search index disagreed with its table; rebuilding it');
    try {
      rebuildKnowledgeIndex(db, index);
      rebuilt.push(index);
    } catch (error) {
      log.error(
        { err: error, index },
        'rebuilding the knowledge search index failed; search over it fails until it is repaired',
      );
      failed.push(index);
    }
  }
  return { rebuilt, failed };
}

/** The flag card for an index that could not be rebuilt. */
export function brokenIndexFlag(index: KnowledgeIndex): {
  title: string;
  body: string;
  foldKey: string;
} {
  return {
    title: 'Knowledge search is broken',
    body: `10db could not rebuild the ${index} search index at start, so knowledge search fails until it is repaired, and session briefings carry the newest decisions instead of the ones that match the task. daemon.log has the error. 10db tries again at every start; restoring a backup from the data folder also repairs it.`,
    foldKey: `knowledge-index:${index}`,
  };
}
```

```ts
// An index that could not be rebuilt is flagged once the inbox exists. A card needs a project, and
// every project's knowledge search is affected, so each project gets one, folded across restarts.
for (const index of knowledgeIndex.failed)
  for (const project of listProjects(db))
    inbox.flag({ projectId: project.id, workstreamId: null, ...brokenIndexFlag(index) });
```

```ts
const KNOWLEDGE_PARAMS = ['projectId', 'scope', 'area', 'kind', 'q', 'history'] as const;
const PROPOSAL_PARAMS = ['projectId', 'status'] as const;

/** The named query parameters as strings; the handler parses them with the route's schema. */
function stringParams<const K extends string>(keys: readonly K[]) {
  return (query: Record<string, string | string[]>): Partial<Record<K, string>> => {
    const out: Partial<Record<K, string>> = {};
    for (const key of keys) {
      const value = query[key];
      if (typeof value === 'string') out[key] = value;
    }
    return out;
  };
}
```

```ts
import { mintId, type DecisionDto, type PrincipleDto, type ProposalDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { baseHandlers, daemonUrl, renderApp, server } from '../../test/daemon';
import {
  decisionDto,
  principleDto,
  projectDto,
  proposalDto,
  provenanceDto,
} from '../../test/fixtures';

const project = projectDto({
  areas: [
    { name: 'api', globs: ['src/api/**'] },
    { name: 'ui', globs: ['src/ui/**'] },
  ],
});

/** Serves one project's knowledge and records every list request's query string. */
function serve(
  data: { principles?: PrincipleDto[]; decisions?: DecisionDto[]; proposals?: ProposalDto[] } = {},
) {
  const asked: URLSearchParams[] = [];
  server.use(
    ...baseHandlers(),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/projects'), () => HttpResponse.json([project])),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/principles'), ({ request }) => {
      asked.push(new URL(request.url).searchParams);
      return HttpResponse.json(data.principles ?? []);
    }),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/decisions'), ({ request }) => {
      asked.push(new URL(request.url).searchParams);
      return HttpResponse.json(data.decisions ?? []);
    }),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/proposals'), () => HttpResponse.json(data.proposals ?? [])),
  );
  return { asked };
}

describe('KnowledgeView', () => {
  it('asks for a project first', async () => {
    server.use(...baseHandlers());
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Knowledge', level: 2 })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Create a project on the board first.')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('is in the navigation between Inbox and Settings', async () => {
    serve();
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    const links = (await screen.findAllByRole('link')).map((l) => l.textContent);
    expect(links.indexOf('Knowledge')).toBeGreaterThan(links.indexOf('Inbox'));
    expect(links.indexOf('Knowledge')).toBeLessThan(links.indexOf('Settings'));
  });

  it('lists principles policies first with their key, scope and state, and searches as you type', async () => {
    const { asked } = serve({
      principles: [
        principleDto({
          statement: 'Prefer small PRs',
          kind: 'heuristic',
          topicKey: 'prs.small',
          scope: 'you',
          projectId: null,
        }),
        principleDto({ statement: 'Never sleep in tests', scope: 'area', area: 'api' }),
      ],
    });
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Principles' });
    const rows = within(table).getAllByRole('row').slice(1);
    expect(rows.map((r) => within(r).getAllByRole('cell')[1]?.textContent)).toEqual([
      'Never sleep in tests',
      'Prefer small PRs',
    ]);
    expect(within(rows[0] ?? table).getByText('Area: api')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(rows[1] ?? table).getByText('You')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Search' }), {
      target: { value: 'sleep' },
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(asked.at(-1)?.get('q')).toBe('sleep');
    });
    expect(asked.at(-1)?.get('projectId')).toBe(project.id);
  });

  it('filters by scope and area, and shows history with each record’s state', async () => {
    const { asked } = serve({
      principles: [
        principleDto({ state: 'superseded', status: 'confirmed' }),
        principleDto({ state: 'withdrawn', withdrawnReason: 'typo' }),
      ],
    });
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Principles' });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Area' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(asked.at(-1)?.get('scope')).toBe('area');
    });
    expect(asked.at(-1)?.get('area')).toBe('api');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Show history' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(asked.at(-1)?.get('history')).toBe('1');
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Superseded')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('Withdrawn')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('lists decisions on their own tab', async () => {
    serve({
      decisions: [
        decisionDto({ scope: 'area', area: 'ui', question: 'Which icons?', choice: 'Tabler' }),
      ],
    });
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Decisions' }));
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Decisions' });
    expect(within(table).getByText('Which icons?')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(table).getByText('Tabler')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(table).getByText('Area: ui')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('counts pending proposals on their tab and shows what each proposes', async () => {
    serve({ proposals: [proposalDto()] });
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Proposals (1)' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('No sleeps in tests')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('Never sleep in tests')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('Two flaky runs came from sleeps.')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('shows where a principle came from', async () => {
    const principle = principleDto({
      speaker: 'agent:ses_1',
      sourceQuote: 'sleeping made the suite flaky',
    });
    serve({ principles: [principle] });
    server.use(
      http.get(daemonUrl(`/principles/${principle.id}/provenance`), () =>
        HttpResponse.json(
          provenanceDto({
            sourceKind: 'agent',
            by: 'agent:ses_1',
            session: {
              id: mintId('Session'),
              workstreamId: mintId('Workstream'),
              taskTitle: 'Fix the flaky suite',
            },
            quote: 'sleeping made the suite flaky',
            confirmedBy: 'me',
            confirmedAt: '2026-10-13T12:00:00.000Z',
          }),
        ),
      ),
    );
    renderApp('/knowledge');
    fireEvent.click(
      await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Where testing.no-sleeps came from' }),
    );
    const drawer = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Where it came from' });
    expect(within(drawer).getByText('An agent proposed it')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(drawer).getByText('Fix the flaky suite')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(drawer).getByText('sleeping made the suite flaky')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`apps/desktop/src/daemon/knowledge-queries.ts`:

```ts
import {
  DecisionDto,
  PrincipleDto,
  ProposalDto,
  ProvenanceDto,
  type DecisionId,
  type KnowledgeScope,
  type PrincipleId,
  type ProjectId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { readJson } from './client';
import { useDaemon } from './provider';

export interface KnowledgeFilter {
  readonly projectId: ProjectId;
  readonly scope: KnowledgeScope | null;
  readonly area: string | null;
  readonly q: string;
  readonly history: boolean;
}

/** Query keys; `decision`, `principle` and `proposal` events invalidate by their first element. */
export const knowledgeKeys = {
  decisions: (f: KnowledgeFilter) => ['decisions', f] as const,
  principles: (f: KnowledgeFilter) => ['principles', f] as const,
  proposals: (projectId: ProjectId) => ['proposals', projectId] as const,
  provenance: (id: DecisionId | PrincipleId) => ['provenance', id] as const,
};

function params(f: KnowledgeFilter) {
  const q = f.q.trim();
  return {
    projectId: f.projectId,
    history: f.history ? '1' : '0',
    ...(f.scope ? { scope: f.scope } : {}),
    ...(f.area ? { area: f.area } : {}),
    ...(q ? { q } : {}),
  };
}

const Decisions = z.array(DecisionDto);
const Principles = z.array(PrincipleDto);
const Proposals = z.array(ProposalDto);

export function useDecisions(f: KnowledgeFilter) {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: knowledgeKeys.decisions(f),
    queryFn: async () => readJson(await client.decisions.$get({ query: params(f) }), Decisions),
  });
}

export function usePrinciples(f: KnowledgeFilter) {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: knowledgeKeys.principles(f),
    queryFn: async () => readJson(await client.principles.$get({ query: params(f) }), Principles),
  });
}

/** Pending proposals of the project; the Proposals tab and its count share it. */
export function useProposals(projectId: ProjectId) {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: knowledgeKeys.proposals(projectId),
    queryFn: async () => readJson(await client.proposals.$get({ query: { projectId } }), Proposals),
  });
}

export type TracedRecord =
  | { readonly kind: 'decision'; readonly id: DecisionId; readonly label: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'principle'; readonly id: PrincipleId; readonly label: string };

/** Where a record came from; it never changes, so it is fetched once per record. */
export function useProvenance(record: TracedRecord | null) {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: record ? knowledgeKeys.provenance(record.id) : ['provenance', 'none'],
    enabled: record !== null,
    staleTime: Infinity,
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (record === null) return null;
      const res =
        record.kind === 'decision'
          ? await client.decisions[':id'].provenance.$get({ param: { id: record.id } })
          : await client.principles[':id'].provenance.$get({ param: { id: record.id } });
      return readJson(res, ProvenanceDto);
    },
  });
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/knowledge-labels.ts`:

```ts
import {
  KnowledgeScope,
  type KnowledgeState,
  type PrincipleKind,
  type SourceKind,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import type { MantineColor } from '@mantine/core';

export const SCOPE_LABELS: Readonly<Record<KnowledgeScope, string>> = {
  you: 'You',
  project: 'Project',
  area: 'Area',
};

export const PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS: Readonly<Record<PrincipleKind, string>> = {
  policy: 'Policy',
  heuristic: 'Heuristic',
  belief: 'Belief',
};

/** Policies first, then heuristics, then beliefs: the order the context pack uses. */
export const PRINCIPLE_KIND_ORDER: readonly PrincipleKind[] = ['policy', 'heuristic', 'belief'];

export const STATE_LABELS: Readonly<Record<KnowledgeState, string>> = {
  proposed: 'Proposed',
  current: 'Current',
  superseded: 'Superseded',
  retired: 'Retired',
  withdrawn: 'Withdrawn',
};

// DESIGN.md: yellow waits on a human, green is done, gray is out of play, red is flagged.
export const STATE_COLORS: Readonly<Record<KnowledgeState, MantineColor>> = {
  proposed: 'yellow',
  current: 'green',
  superseded: 'gray',
  retired: 'gray',
  withdrawn: 'red',
};

export const SOURCE_LABELS: Readonly<Record<SourceKind, string>> = {
  builder: 'You recorded it in 10db',
  inbox_answer: 'Your answer in the inbox',
  agent: 'An agent proposed it',
  daemon: '10db recorded it',
};

export function scopeText(r: {
  readonly scope: KnowledgeScope;
  readonly area: string | null;
}): string {
  return r.scope === KnowledgeScope.Area && r.area !== null
    ? `Area: ${r.area}`
    : SCOPE_LABELS[r.scope];
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/knowledge-filters.tsx`:

```tsx
import { KnowledgeScope } from '@10db/contracts';
import { Group, SegmentedControl, Select, Switch, TextInput } from '@mantine/core';
import { IconSearch } from '@tabler/icons-react';

export interface FilterState {
  readonly scope: KnowledgeScope | null;
  readonly area: string | null;
  readonly q: string;
  readonly history: boolean;
}

export const NO_FILTERS: FilterState = { scope: null, area: null, q: '', history: false };

const ALL = 'all';
const SCOPES: readonly string[] = Object.values(KnowledgeScope);
const isScope = (value: string): value is KnowledgeScope => SCOPES.includes(value);

export function KnowledgeFilters({
  value,
  areas,
  onChange,
}: {
  readonly value: FilterState;
  readonly areas: readonly string[];
  readonly onChange: (next: FilterState) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Group align="flex-end" gap="sm">
      <TextInput
        label="Search"
        size="sm"
        w={260}
        leftSection={<IconSearch size={16} stroke={1.5} />}
        value={value.q}
        onChange={(e) => {
          onChange({ ...value, q: e.currentTarget.value });
        }}
      />
      <SegmentedControl
        aria-label="Scope"
        size="sm"
        value={value.scope ?? ALL}
        data={[
          { value: ALL, label: 'All in scope' },
          { value: KnowledgeScope.You, label: 'You' },
          { value: KnowledgeScope.Project, label: 'Project' },
          { value: KnowledgeScope.Area, label: 'Area' },
        ]}
        onChange={(next) => {
          const scope = isScope(next) ? next : null;
          onChange({
            ...value,
            scope,
            area: scope === KnowledgeScope.Area ? (value.area ?? areas[0] ?? null) : null,
          });
        }}
      />
      {value.scope === KnowledgeScope.Area ? (
        <Select
          label="Area"
          size="sm"
          w={160}
          data={[...areas]}
          value={value.area}
          allowDeselect={false}
          onChange={(area) => {
            onChange({ ...value, area });
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
      <Switch
        label="Show history"
        checked={value.history}
        onChange={(e) => {
          onChange({ ...value, history: e.currentTarget.checked });
        }}
      />
    </Group>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/principle-list.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { PrincipleDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { ActionIcon, Alert, Badge, Code, Group, Table, Text, VisuallyHidden } from '@mantine/core';
import { IconInfoCircle } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  usePrinciples,
  type KnowledgeFilter,
  type TracedRecord,
} from '../../daemon/knowledge-queries';
import {
  PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS,
  PRINCIPLE_KIND_ORDER,
  scopeText,
  STATE_COLORS,
  STATE_LABELS,
} from './knowledge-labels';

const byKind = (a: PrincipleDto, b: PrincipleDto): number =>
  PRINCIPLE_KIND_ORDER.indexOf(a.kind) - PRINCIPLE_KIND_ORDER.indexOf(b.kind);

export function PrincipleList({
  filter,
  onTrace,
  actions,
}: {
  readonly filter: KnowledgeFilter;
  readonly onTrace: (record: TracedRecord) => void;
  readonly actions?: (principle: PrincipleDto) => ReactNode;
}) {
  const principles = usePrinciples(filter);
  if (principles.isError)
    return (
      <Alert color="red" title="Couldn't load the principles">
        {principles.error.message}
      </Alert>
    );
  const rows = (principles.data ?? []).toSorted(byKind);
  if (principles.isSuccess && rows.length === 0)
    return (
      <Text c="dimmed">
        {filter.q.trim()
          ? 'No principle matches.'
          : 'No principles yet. Agents propose them; you confirm them here or in the Inbox.'}
      </Text>
    );
  return (
    <Table aria-label="Principles" verticalSpacing="xs">
      <Table.Thead>
        <Table.Tr>
          <Table.Th>Key</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>Statement</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>Kind</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>Scope</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>State</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>
            <VisuallyHidden>Actions</VisuallyHidden>
          </Table.Th>
        </Table.Tr>
      </Table.Thead>
      <Table.Tbody>
        {rows.map((p) => (
          <Table.Tr key={p.id}>
            <Table.Td>
              <Code>{p.topicKey}</Code>
            </Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>{p.statement}</Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>{PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS[p.kind]}</Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>{scopeText(p)}</Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>
              <Badge size="sm" variant="light" color={STATE_COLORS[p.state]}>
                {STATE_LABELS[p.state]}
              </Badge>
            </Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>
              <Group gap={4} wrap="nowrap" justify="flex-end">
                <ActionIcon
                  variant="subtle"
                  size="sm"
                  aria-label={`Where ${p.topicKey} came from`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onTrace({ kind: 'principle', id: p.id, label: p.statement });
                  }}
                >
                  <IconInfoCircle size={16} stroke={1.5} />
                </ActionIcon>
                {actions?.(p)}
              </Group>
            </Table.Td>
          </Table.Tr>
        ))}
      </Table.Tbody>
    </Table>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/decision-list.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { DecisionDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { ActionIcon, Alert, Badge, Group, Table, Text, VisuallyHidden } from '@mantine/core';
import { IconInfoCircle } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  useDecisions,
  type KnowledgeFilter,
  type TracedRecord,
} from '../../daemon/knowledge-queries';
import { scopeText, STATE_COLORS, STATE_LABELS } from './knowledge-labels';

/** Newest first, as the daemon sends them; with a query, best match first. */
export function DecisionList({
  filter,
  onTrace,
  actions,
}: {
  readonly filter: KnowledgeFilter;
  readonly onTrace: (record: TracedRecord) => void;
  readonly actions?: (decision: DecisionDto) => ReactNode;
}) {
  const decisions = useDecisions(filter);
  if (decisions.isError)
    return (
      <Alert color="red" title="Couldn't load the decisions">
        {decisions.error.message}
      </Alert>
    );
  const rows = decisions.data ?? [];
  if (decisions.isSuccess && rows.length === 0)
    return (
      <Text c="dimmed">
        {filter.q.trim()
          ? 'No decision matches.'
          : "No decisions yet. Answers in the inbox and the agents' routine choices are recorded here."}
      </Text>
    );
  return (
    <Table aria-label="Decisions" verticalSpacing="xs">
      <Table.Thead>
        <Table.Tr>
          <Table.Th>Question</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>Choice</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>Rationale</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>Scope</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>State</Table.Th>
          <Table.Th>
            <VisuallyHidden>Actions</VisuallyHidden>
          </Table.Th>
        </Table.Tr>
      </Table.Thead>
      <Table.Tbody>
        {rows.map((d) => (
          <Table.Tr key={d.id}>
            <Table.Td>{d.question}</Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>{d.choice}</Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>
              <Text size="sm" lineClamp={2}>
                {d.rationale}
              </Text>
            </Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>{scopeText(d)}</Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>
              <Badge size="sm" variant="light" color={STATE_COLORS[d.state]}>
                {STATE_LABELS[d.state]}
              </Badge>
            </Table.Td>
            <Table.Td>
              <Group gap={4} wrap="nowrap" justify="flex-end">
                <ActionIcon
                  variant="subtle"
                  size="sm"
                  aria-label={`Where "${d.question}" came from`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onTrace({ kind: 'decision', id: d.id, label: d.question });
                  }}
                >
                  <IconInfoCircle size={16} stroke={1.5} />
                </ActionIcon>
                {actions?.(d)}
              </Group>
            </Table.Td>
          </Table.Tr>
        ))}
      </Table.Tbody>
    </Table>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/proposal-list.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ProjectDto, ProposalDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { Alert, Badge, Code, Group, Paper, Stack, Text } from '@mantine/core';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useProposals } from '../../daemon/knowledge-queries';
import { PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS, scopeText } from './knowledge-labels';

/** Proposal text comes from agents and answers: it is rendered as text only (spec §7). */
export function ProposalList({
  project,
  actions,
}: {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly actions?: (proposal: ProposalDto) => ReactNode;
}) {
  const proposals = useProposals(project.id);
  if (proposals.isError)
    return (
      <Alert color="red" title="Couldn't load the proposals">
        {proposals.error.message}
      </Alert>
    );
  const list = proposals.data ?? [];
  if (proposals.isSuccess && list.length === 0)
    return <Text c="dimmed">No proposals wait for you.</Text>;
  return (
    <Stack gap="xs">
      {list.map((proposal) => (
        <Paper key={proposal.id} withBorder p="sm">
          <Stack gap={6}>
            <Group justify="space-between" wrap="nowrap">
              <Text fw={600}>{proposal.title}</Text>
              {actions?.(proposal)}
            </Group>
            {proposal.principle ? (
              <Group gap="xs">
                <Badge size="sm" variant="light">
                  {PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS[proposal.principle.kind]}
                </Badge>
                <Code>{proposal.principle.topicKey}</Code>
                <Text size="sm">{proposal.principle.statement}</Text>
                <Text size="xs" c="dimmed">
                  Proposed for: {scopeText(proposal.principle)}
                </Text>
              </Group>
            ) : null}
            <Text size="sm" c="dimmed" style={{ whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap' }}>
              {proposal.body}
            </Text>
          </Stack>
        </Paper>
      ))}
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/provenance-drawer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Alert, Blockquote, Drawer, Stack, Text } from '@mantine/core';
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { useProvenance, type TracedRecord } from '../../daemon/knowledge-queries';
import { SOURCE_LABELS } from './knowledge-labels';

const when = (iso: string): string => dayjs(iso).format('D MMM YYYY, HH:mm');

export function ProvenanceDrawer({
  record,
  onClose,
}: {
  readonly record: TracedRecord | null;
  readonly onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const provenance = useProvenance(record);
  const p = provenance.data ?? null;
  return (
    <Drawer opened={record !== null} onClose={onClose} position="right" title="Where it came from">
      <Stack gap="sm">
        {record ? <Text fw={600}>{record.label}</Text> : null}
        {provenance.isError ? (
          <Alert color="red" title="Couldn't load where it came from">
            {provenance.error.message}
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        {p ? (
          <>
            <Text>{SOURCE_LABELS[p.sourceKind]}</Text>
            <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">
              Recorded {when(p.recordedAt)} by {p.by}
            </Text>
            {p.inbox ? (
              <Stack gap={2}>
                <Text size="sm">Card: {p.inbox.title}</Text>
                {p.inbox.choice ? <Text size="sm">Answer: {p.inbox.choice}</Text> : null}
                {p.inbox.note ? <Text size="sm">Note: {p.inbox.note}</Text> : null}
              </Stack>
            ) : null}
            {p.session ? (
              <Stack gap={2}>
                <Text size="sm">Task</Text>
                <Text size="sm">{p.session.taskTitle ?? p.session.workstreamId}</Text>
              </Stack>
            ) : null}
            {p.quote ? <Blockquote>{p.quote}</Blockquote> : null}
            {p.confirmedBy && p.confirmedAt ? (
              <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">
                Confirmed by {p.confirmedBy} on {when(p.confirmedAt)}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
          </>
        ) : null}
      </Stack>
    </Drawer>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/knowledge-view.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Group, Stack, Tabs, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useDebouncedValue } from '@mantine/hooks';
import { useState } from 'react';
import {
  useProposals,
  type KnowledgeFilter,
  type TracedRecord,
} from '../../daemon/knowledge-queries';
import { ProjectPicker } from '../../shell/project-picker';
import { useSelectedProject } from '../../shell/selected-project';
import type { ProjectDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { DecisionList } from './decision-list';
import { KnowledgeFilters, NO_FILTERS, type FilterState } from './knowledge-filters';
import { PrincipleList } from './principle-list';
import { ProposalList } from './proposal-list';
import { ProvenanceDrawer } from './provenance-drawer';

/** Long enough to skip a request per keystroke, short enough to feel live. */
const SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 250;

function ProposalsTabLabel({ project }: { readonly project: ProjectDto }) {
  const count = useProposals(project.id).data?.length ?? 0;
  return <>{count > 0 ? `Proposals (${String(count)})` : 'Proposals'}</>;
}

function ProjectKnowledge({ project }: { readonly project: ProjectDto }) {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterState>(NO_FILTERS);
  const [q] = useDebouncedValue(filters.q, SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS);
  const [traced, setTraced] = useState<TracedRecord | null>(null);
  const filter: KnowledgeFilter = {
    projectId: project.id,
    scope: filters.scope,
    area: filters.area,
    q,
    history: filters.history,
  };
  const areas = project.areas.map((a) => a.name);
  return (
    <>
      <Tabs defaultValue="principles" keepMounted={false}>
        <Tabs.List>
          <Tabs.Tab value="principles">Principles</Tabs.Tab>
          <Tabs.Tab value="decisions">Decisions</Tabs.Tab>
          <Tabs.Tab value="proposals">
            <ProposalsTabLabel project={project} />
          </Tabs.Tab>
        </Tabs.List>
        <Tabs.Panel value="principles" pt="md">
          <Stack>
            <KnowledgeFilters value={filters} areas={areas} onChange={setFilters} />
            <PrincipleList filter={filter} onTrace={setTraced} />
          </Stack>
        </Tabs.Panel>
        <Tabs.Panel value="decisions" pt="md">
          <Stack>
            <KnowledgeFilters value={filters} areas={areas} onChange={setFilters} />
            <DecisionList filter={filter} onTrace={setTraced} />
          </Stack>
        </Tabs.Panel>
        <Tabs.Panel value="proposals" pt="md">
          <ProposalList project={project} />
        </Tabs.Panel>
      </Tabs>
      <ProvenanceDrawer
        record={traced}
        onClose={() => {
          setTraced(null);
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

/** Spec §10 Knowledge: decisions, principles and pending proposals of one project, filtered by scope. */
export function KnowledgeView() {
  const { project, select } = useSelectedProject();
  return (
    <Stack>
      <Group justify="space-between" align="flex-end">
        <Title order={2}>Knowledge</Title>
        {project ? <ProjectPicker value={project.id} onChange={select} /> : null}
      </Group>
      {project ? (
        <ProjectKnowledge key={project.id} project={project} />
      ) : (
        <Text c="dimmed">Create a project on the board first.</Text>
      )}
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { baseHandlers, daemonUrl, renderApp, server } from '../../test/daemon';
import { decisionDto, principleDto, projectDto, proposalDto } from '../../test/fixtures';

const project = projectDto({ areas: [{ name: 'api', globs: ['src/api/**'] }] });

function serve(over: Parameters<typeof server.use> = []) {
  const posted: { path: string; body: unknown }[] = [];
  const record =
    (status: number, reply: unknown) =>
    async ({ request }: { request: Request }) => {
      posted.push({ path: new URL(request.url).pathname, body: await request.json() });
      return HttpResponse.json(reply, { status });
    };
  server.use(
    ...baseHandlers(),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/projects'), () => HttpResponse.json([project])),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/principles'), () =>
      HttpResponse.json([principleDto({ projectId: project.id })]),
    ),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/decisions'), () =>
      HttpResponse.json([decisionDto({ projectId: project.id })]),
    ),
    http.get(daemonUrl('/proposals'), () =>
      HttpResponse.json([proposalDto({ projectId: project.id })]),
    ),
    http.post(daemonUrl('/decisions'), record(201, decisionDto())),
    http.post(daemonUrl('/decisions/:id/supersede'), record(201, decisionDto())),
    http.post(
      daemonUrl('/decisions/:id/withdraw'),
      record(200, decisionDto({ state: 'withdrawn' })),
    ),
    http.post(daemonUrl('/principles'), record(201, principleDto())),
    http.post(
      daemonUrl('/principles/:id/retire'),
      record(200, principleDto({ status: 'retired', state: 'retired' })),
    ),
    http.post(daemonUrl('/proposals/:id/accept'), record(200, proposalDto({ status: 'accepted' }))),
    http.post(daemonUrl('/proposals/:id/reject'), record(200, proposalDto({ status: 'rejected' }))),
    ...over,
  );
  return { posted };
}

describe('knowledge capture', () => {
```

```ts
/** Every knowledge write can change all three lists and close an inbox card. */
function useKnowledgeWrite<V, R>(mutationFn: (variables: V) => Promise<R>) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn,
    onSettled: () =>
      Promise.all(
        [['decisions'], ['principles'], ['proposals'], queryKeys.inbox].map((queryKey) =>
          queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }),
        ),
      ),
  });
}

export function useRecordDecision() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(async (input: DecisionInput) =>
    readJson(await client.decisions.$post({ json: input }), DecisionDto),
  );
}

export function useSupersedeDecision() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(
    async ({ id, input }: { readonly id: DecisionId; readonly input: DecisionInput }) =>
      readJson(
        await client.decisions[':id'].supersede.$post({ param: { id }, json: input }),
        DecisionDto,
      ),
  );
}

export function useWithdrawDecision() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(
    async ({ id, reason }: { readonly id: DecisionId; readonly reason: string }) =>
      readJson(
        await client.decisions[':id'].withdraw.$post({ param: { id }, json: { reason } }),
        DecisionDto,
      ),
  );
}

export function useCreatePrinciple() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(async (input: PrincipleInput) =>
    readJson(await client.principles.$post({ json: input }), PrincipleDto),
  );
}

export function useRetirePrinciple() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(
    async ({ id, reason }: { readonly id: PrincipleId; readonly reason: string }) =>
      readJson(
        await client.principles[':id'].retire.$post({ param: { id }, json: { reason } }),
        PrincipleDto,
      ),
  );
}

export function useWithdrawPrinciple() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(
    async ({ id, reason }: { readonly id: PrincipleId; readonly reason: string }) =>
      readJson(
        await client.principles[':id'].withdraw.$post({ param: { id }, json: { reason } }),
        PrincipleDto,
      ),
  );
}

export function useAcceptProposal() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(
    async ({ id, input }: { readonly id: ProposalId; readonly input: ProposalAccept }) =>
      readJson(
        await client.proposals[':id'].accept.$post({ param: { id }, json: input }),
        ProposalDto,
      ),
  );
}

export function useRejectProposal() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  return useKnowledgeWrite(
    async ({ id, input }: { readonly id: ProposalId; readonly input: ProposalReject }) =>
      readJson(
        await client.proposals[':id'].reject.$post({ param: { id }, json: input }),
        ProposalDto,
      ),
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/target-select.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  KnowledgeScope,
  type KnowledgeTarget,
  type ProjectDto,
  type ProjectId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { Select } from '@mantine/core';

const AREA_PREFIX = 'area:';

export function targetValue(target: KnowledgeTarget): string {
  switch (target.scope) {
    case KnowledgeScope.You:
      return KnowledgeScope.You;
    case KnowledgeScope.Project:
      return KnowledgeScope.Project;
    case KnowledgeScope.Area:
      return `${AREA_PREFIX}${target.area}`;
  }
}

export function targetFrom(value: string, projectId: ProjectId): KnowledgeTarget {
  if (value === KnowledgeScope.You) return { scope: KnowledgeScope.You };
  if (value.startsWith(AREA_PREFIX))
    return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Area, projectId, area: value.slice(AREA_PREFIX.length) };
  return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId };
}

/** The target a stored record has, in the selected project. */
export function recordTarget(
  record: { readonly scope: KnowledgeScope; readonly area: string | null },
  projectId: ProjectId,
): KnowledgeTarget {
  switch (record.scope) {
    case KnowledgeScope.You:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.You };
    case KnowledgeScope.Project:
      return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId };
    case KnowledgeScope.Area:
      return record.area === null
        ? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId }
        : { scope: KnowledgeScope.Area, projectId, area: record.area };
  }
}

export function TargetSelect({
  project,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly value: KnowledgeTarget;
  readonly onChange: (target: KnowledgeTarget) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Select
      label="Applies to"
      allowDeselect={false}
      data={[
        { value: KnowledgeScope.Project, label: `This project (${project.name})` },
        { value: KnowledgeScope.You, label: 'You, in every project' },
        ...project.areas.map((a) => ({
          value: `${AREA_PREFIX}${a.name}`,
          label: `Area: ${a.name}`,
        })),
      ]}
      value={targetValue(value)}
      onChange={(next) => {
        if (next !== null) onChange(targetFrom(next, project.id));
      }}
    />
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/decision-form.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  KnowledgeScope,
  type DecisionInput,
  type KnowledgeTarget,
  type ProjectDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { Alert, Button, Group, Stack, Textarea, TextInput } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type SyntheticEvent } from 'react';
import { TargetSelect } from './target-select';

export interface DecisionFormProps {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly initial?: Omit<DecisionInput, 'fromInboxId'>;
  readonly submitLabel: string;
  readonly pending: boolean;
  readonly error: string | null;
  readonly onSubmit: (input: DecisionInput) => void;
}

export function DecisionForm({
  project,
  initial,
  submitLabel,
  pending,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: DecisionFormProps) {
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState(initial?.question ?? '');
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(initial?.choice ?? '');
  const [rationale, setRationale] = useState(initial?.rationale ?? '');
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<KnowledgeTarget>(
    initial?.target ?? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId: project.id },
  );
  const [missing, setMissing] = useState(false);
  const submit = (event: SyntheticEvent<HTMLFormElement>): void => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const filled = {
      question: question.trim(),
      choice: choice.trim(),
      rationale: rationale.trim(),
    };
    if (!filled.question || !filled.choice || !filled.rationale) {
      setMissing(true);
      return;
    }
    onSubmit({ target, ...filled });
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate>
      <Stack>
        <TextInput
          label="Question"
          value={question}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setQuestion(e.currentTarget.value);
          }}
        />
        <TextInput
          label="Choice"
          value={choice}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setChoice(e.currentTarget.value);
          }}
        />
        <Textarea
          label="Rationale"
          autosize
          minRows={2}
          value={rationale}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setRationale(e.currentTarget.value);
          }}
        />
        <TargetSelect project={project} value={target} onChange={setTarget} />
        {missing ? (
          <Alert color="red" variant="light">
            Fill in the question, the choice and the rationale.
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        {error ? (
          <Alert color="red" variant="light">
            {error}
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Group justify="flex-end">
          <Button type="submit" loading={pending}>
            {submitLabel}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/principle-form.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  KnowledgeScope,
  PrincipleKind,
  topicKeyFrom,
  TopicKey,
  type KnowledgeTarget,
  type PrincipleInput,
  type ProjectDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { Alert, Button, Group, Select, Stack, Textarea, TextInput } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type SyntheticEvent } from 'react';
import { PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS } from './knowledge-labels';
import { TargetSelect } from './target-select';

const KINDS: readonly string[] = Object.values(PrincipleKind);
const isKind = (v: string): v is PrincipleKind => KINDS.includes(v);

export interface PrincipleFormProps {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly initial?: Omit<PrincipleInput, 'quote'>;
  readonly submitLabel: string;
  readonly pending: boolean;
  readonly error: string | null;
  readonly onSubmit: (input: Omit<PrincipleInput, 'quote'>) => void;
}

/** The topic key follows the statement until the builder edits it; a principle with the same key and target replaces the old one. */
export function PrincipleForm({
  project,
  initial,
  submitLabel,
  pending,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: PrincipleFormProps) {
  const [statement, setStatement] = useState(initial?.statement ?? '');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<PrincipleKind>(initial?.kind ?? PrincipleKind.Heuristic);
  const [keyEdited, setKeyEdited] = useState(initial !== undefined);
  const [topicKey, setTopicKey] = useState(initial?.topicKey ?? '');
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<KnowledgeTarget>(
    initial?.target ?? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, projectId: project.id },
  );
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const shownKey = keyEdited ? topicKey : statement.trim() ? topicKeyFrom(statement) : '';
  const submit = (event: SyntheticEvent<HTMLFormElement>): void => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!statement.trim()) {
      setProblem('Write the statement.');
      return;
    }
    const key = TopicKey.safeParse(shownKey);
    if (!key.success) {
      setProblem(
        'The topic key is lowercase words joined by . or - (for example testing.no-sleeps).',
      );
      return;
    }
    setProblem(null);
    onSubmit({ target, statement: statement.trim(), kind, topicKey: key.data });
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate>
      <Stack>
        <Textarea
          label="Statement"
          autosize
          minRows={2}
          value={statement}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setStatement(e.currentTarget.value);
          }}
        />
        <Select
          label="Kind"
          allowDeselect={false}
          data={Object.values(PrincipleKind).map((k) => ({
            value: k,
            label: PRINCIPLE_KIND_LABELS[k],
          }))}
          value={kind}
          onChange={(next) => {
            if (next !== null && isKind(next)) setKind(next);
          }}
        />
        <TextInput
          label="Topic key"
          description="A principle with the same key and scope replaces this one."
          value={shownKey}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setKeyEdited(true);
            setTopicKey(e.currentTarget.value);
          }}
        />
        <TargetSelect project={project} value={target} onChange={setTarget} />
        {problem ? (
          <Alert color="red" variant="light">
            {problem}
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        {error ? (
          <Alert color="red" variant="light">
            {error}
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Group justify="flex-end">
          <Button type="submit" loading={pending}>
            {submitLabel}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/reason-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Alert, Button, Group, Modal, Stack, Textarea } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type SyntheticEvent } from 'react';

export interface ReasonDialogProps {
  readonly title: string;
  readonly submitLabel: string;
  readonly opened: boolean;
  /** Retiring and withdrawing need a reason (spec §9); rejecting a proposal takes an optional note. */
  readonly required: boolean;
  readonly pending: boolean;
  readonly error: string | null;
  readonly onSubmit: (reason: string) => void;
  readonly onClose: () => void;
}

export function ReasonDialog({
  title,
  submitLabel,
  opened,
  required,
  pending,
  error,
  onSubmit,
  onClose,
}: ReasonDialogProps) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [missing, setMissing] = useState(false);
  const submit = (event: SyntheticEvent<HTMLFormElement>): void => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (required && !reason.trim()) {
      setMissing(true);
      return;
    }
    onSubmit(reason.trim());
  };
  return (
    <Modal opened={opened} onClose={onClose} title={title}>
      <form onSubmit={submit} noValidate>
        <Stack>
          <Textarea
            label={required ? 'Reason' : 'Note (optional)'}
            autosize
            minRows={2}
            value={reason}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setReason(e.currentTarget.value);
            }}
          />
          {missing ? (
            <Alert color="red" variant="light">
              Say why.
            </Alert>
          ) : null}
          {error ? (
            <Alert color="red" variant="light">
              {error}
            </Alert>
          ) : null}
          <Group justify="flex-end">
            <Button type="submit" color="red" loading={pending}>
              {submitLabel}
            </Button>
          </Group>
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/proposal-accept-form.tsx` is `PrincipleForm` prefilled from the proposal's principle, submitting `{ target, statement, kind, topicKey }` as the `ProposalAccept`:

```tsx
import type { ProjectDto, ProposalAccept, ProposalDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { PrincipleForm } from './principle-form';
import { recordTarget } from './target-select';

export function ProposalAcceptForm({
  project,
  proposal,
  pending,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly proposal: ProposalDto;
  readonly pending: boolean;
  readonly error: string | null;
  readonly onSubmit: (accept: ProposalAccept) => void;
}) {
  const p = proposal.principle;
  if (!p) return null;
  return (
    <PrincipleForm
      project={project}
      initial={{
        target: recordTarget(p, project.id),
        statement: p.statement,
        kind: p.kind,
        topicKey: p.topicKey,
      }}
      submitLabel="Accept"
      pending={pending}
      error={error}
      onSubmit={onSubmit}
    />
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/record-actions.tsx` holds the row menus and the proposal buttons, each owning its dialog and mutation:

```tsx
import {
  KnowledgeState,
  type DecisionDto,
  type PrincipleDto,
  type ProjectDto,
  type ProposalDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { ActionIcon, Button, Group, Menu, Modal } from '@mantine/core';
import { IconDots } from '@tabler/icons-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { DaemonRequestError } from '../../daemon/client';
import {
  useAcceptProposal,
  useCreatePrinciple,
  useRejectProposal,
  useRetirePrinciple,
  useSupersedeDecision,
  useWithdrawDecision,
  useWithdrawPrinciple,
} from '../../daemon/knowledge-queries';
import { DecisionForm } from './decision-form';
import { PrincipleForm } from './principle-form';
import { ProposalAcceptForm } from './proposal-accept-form';
import { ReasonDialog } from './reason-dialog';
import { recordTarget } from './target-select';

const KnowledgeAction = {
  None: 'none',
  Supersede: 'supersede',
  Edit: 'edit',
  Retire: 'retire',
  Withdraw: 'withdraw',
} as const;
type KnowledgeAction = (typeof KnowledgeAction)[keyof typeof KnowledgeAction];

/** A conflict means someone decided first; the lists refetch on settle, so say what happened. */
export function writeError(error: Error | null, conflict: string): string | null {
  if (error === null) return null;
  return error instanceof DaemonRequestError && error.status === 409 ? conflict : error.message;
}

export function DecisionActions({
  project,
  decision,
}: {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly decision: DecisionDto;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<KnowledgeAction>(KnowledgeAction.None);
  const supersede = useSupersedeDecision();
  const withdraw = useWithdrawDecision();
  const close = () => {
    setOpen(KnowledgeAction.None);
  };
  if (decision.state !== KnowledgeState.Current) return null;
  const target = recordTarget(decision, project.id);
  return (
    <>
      <Menu position="bottom-end">
        <Menu.Target>
          <ActionIcon variant="subtle" size="sm" aria-label={`Actions for "${decision.question}"`}>
            <IconDots size={16} stroke={1.5} />
          </ActionIcon>
        </Menu.Target>
        <Menu.Dropdown>
          <Menu.Item
            onClick={() => {
              setOpen(KnowledgeAction.Supersede);
            }}
          >
            Supersede
          </Menu.Item>
          <Menu.Item
            color="red"
            onClick={() => {
              setOpen(KnowledgeAction.Withdraw);
            }}
          >
            Withdraw
          </Menu.Item>
        </Menu.Dropdown>
      </Menu>
      <Modal
        opened={open === KnowledgeAction.Supersede}
        onClose={close}
        title="Supersede the decision"
      >
        <DecisionForm
          project={project}
          initial={{
            target,
            question: decision.question,
            choice: decision.choice,
            rationale: decision.rationale,
          }}
          submitLabel="Supersede"
          pending={supersede.isPending}
          error={writeError(
            supersede.error,
            'This decision changed meanwhile; the list is refreshed.',
          )}
          onSubmit={(input) => {
            supersede.mutate({ id: decision.id, input }, { onSuccess: close });
          }}
        />
      </Modal>
      <ReasonDialog
        title="Withdraw the decision"
        submitLabel="Withdraw"
        opened={open === KnowledgeAction.Withdraw}
        required
        pending={withdraw.isPending}
        error={writeError(
          withdraw.error,
          'This decision is no longer current; the list is refreshed.',
        )}
        onSubmit={(reason) => {
          withdraw.mutate({ id: decision.id, reason }, { onSuccess: close });
        }}
        onClose={close}
      />
    </>
  );
}
```

Append the principle and proposal actions to the same file:

```tsx
export function PrincipleActions({
  project,
  principle,
}: {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly principle: PrincipleDto;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<KnowledgeAction>(KnowledgeAction.None);
  const edit = useCreatePrinciple();
  const retire = useRetirePrinciple();
  const withdraw = useWithdrawPrinciple();
  const close = () => {
    setOpen(KnowledgeAction.None);
  };
  if (principle.state !== KnowledgeState.Current) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <Menu position="bottom-end">
        <Menu.Target>
          <ActionIcon variant="subtle" size="sm" aria-label={`Actions for ${principle.topicKey}`}>
            <IconDots size={16} stroke={1.5} />
          </ActionIcon>
        </Menu.Target>
        <Menu.Dropdown>
          <Menu.Item
            onClick={() => {
              setOpen(KnowledgeAction.Edit);
            }}
          >
            Edit
          </Menu.Item>
          <Menu.Item
            onClick={() => {
              setOpen(KnowledgeAction.Retire);
            }}
          >
            Retire
          </Menu.Item>
          <Menu.Item
            color="red"
            onClick={() => {
              setOpen(KnowledgeAction.Withdraw);
            }}
          >
            Withdraw
          </Menu.Item>
        </Menu.Dropdown>
      </Menu>
      {/* Saving writes a new confirmed principle with the same key and target, which supersedes this one. */}
      <Modal opened={open === KnowledgeAction.Edit} onClose={close} title="Edit the principle">
        <PrincipleForm
          project={project}
          initial={{
            target: recordTarget(principle, project.id),
            statement: principle.statement,
            kind: principle.kind,
            topicKey: principle.topicKey,
          }}
          submitLabel="Save"
          pending={edit.isPending}
          error={writeError(edit.error, 'This principle changed meanwhile; the list is refreshed.')}
          onSubmit={(input) => {
            edit.mutate(input, { onSuccess: close });
          }}
        />
      </Modal>
      <ReasonDialog
        title="Retire the principle"
        submitLabel="Retire"
        opened={open === KnowledgeAction.Retire}
        required
        pending={retire.isPending}
        error={writeError(
          retire.error,
          'This principle is no longer current; the list is refreshed.',
        )}
        onSubmit={(reason) => {
          retire.mutate({ id: principle.id, reason }, { onSuccess: close });
        }}
        onClose={close}
      />
      <ReasonDialog
        title="Withdraw the principle"
        submitLabel="Withdraw"
        opened={open === KnowledgeAction.Withdraw}
        required
        pending={withdraw.isPending}
        error={writeError(
          withdraw.error,
          'This principle is no longer current; the list is refreshed.',
        )}
        onSubmit={(reason) => {
          withdraw.mutate({ id: principle.id, reason }, { onSuccess: close });
        }}
        onClose={close}
      />
    </>
  );
}

const PROPOSAL_CONFLICT = 'This proposal was already decided; the list is refreshed.';
const ProposalDialog = { None: 'none', Accept: 'accept', Reject: 'reject' } as const;
type ProposalDialog = (typeof ProposalDialog)[keyof typeof ProposalDialog];

export function ProposalActions({
  project,
  proposal,
}: {
  readonly project: ProjectDto;
  readonly proposal: ProposalDto;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<ProposalDialog>(ProposalDialog.None);
  const accept = useAcceptProposal();
  const reject = useRejectProposal();
  const close = () => {
    setOpen(ProposalDialog.None);
  };
  return (
    <Group gap="xs" wrap="nowrap">
      <Button
        size="xs"
        loading={accept.isPending && open === ProposalDialog.None}
        onClick={() => {
          if (proposal.principle) setOpen(ProposalDialog.Accept);
          else accept.mutate({ id: proposal.id, input: {} });
        }}
      >
        Accept…
      </Button>
      <Button
        size="xs"
        variant="default"
        onClick={() => {
          setOpen(ProposalDialog.Reject);
        }}
      >
        Reject
      </Button>
      <Modal opened={open === ProposalDialog.Accept} onClose={close} title="Accept the proposal">
        <ProposalAcceptForm
          project={project}
          proposal={proposal}
          pending={accept.isPending}
          error={writeError(accept.error, PROPOSAL_CONFLICT)}
          onSubmit={(input) => {
            accept.mutate({ id: proposal.id, input }, { onSuccess: close });
          }}
        />
      </Modal>
      <ReasonDialog
        title="Reject the proposal"
        submitLabel="Reject"
        opened={open === ProposalDialog.Reject}
        required={false}
        pending={reject.isPending}
        error={writeError(reject.error, PROPOSAL_CONFLICT)}
        onSubmit={(note) => {
          reject.mutate(
            { id: proposal.id, input: note === '' ? {} : { note } },
            { onSuccess: close },
          );
        }}
        onClose={close}
      />
    </Group>
  );
}
```
