In `apps/desktop/src/views/inbox/permission-labels.ts`:

```ts
export const PRINCIPLE_VERDICT_LABELS: Readonly<Record<PrincipleVerdict, string>> = {
  [PrincipleVerdict.AcceptProject]: 'Accept for this project',
  [PrincipleVerdict.AcceptYou]: 'Accept for all my projects',
  [PrincipleVerdict.AcceptArea]: 'Accept for its area',
  [PrincipleVerdict.Reject]: 'Reject',
};

const isPrincipleCard = (item: InboxItemDto): boolean =>
  item.kind === InboxKind.Proposal && typeof item.detail?.['principleId'] === 'string';

function isPrincipleVerdict(label: string): label is PrincipleVerdict {
  return Object.hasOwn(PRINCIPLE_VERDICT_LABELS, label);
}
```

and in `optionLabel`, before the final return:

```ts
if (isPrincipleCard(item) && isPrincipleVerdict(option.label))
  return PRINCIPLE_VERDICT_LABELS[option.label];
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the record control and send it**

`apps/desktop/src/views/inbox/record-decision.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  DECISION_CATEGORIES,
  InboxKind,
  KnowledgeScope,
  type DecisionRecording,
  type InboxItemDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { Checkbox, Group, Select } from '@mantine/core';
import { useProjects } from '../../daemon/queries';

const AREA_PREFIX = 'area:';

/** The daemon's own default (spec §7): product, design and engineering answers are project decisions. */
export function defaultRecording(item: InboxItemDto): DecisionRecording | null {
  if (item.kind !== InboxKind.Question) return null;
  return DECISION_CATEGORIES.includes(item.category)
    ? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, area: null }
    : null;
}

const valueOf = (r: DecisionRecording): string =>
  r.scope === KnowledgeScope.Area && r.area !== null ? `${AREA_PREFIX}${r.area}` : r.scope;

function recordingOf(value: string): DecisionRecording {
  if (value.startsWith(AREA_PREFIX))
    return { scope: KnowledgeScope.Area, area: value.slice(AREA_PREFIX.length) };
  return value === KnowledgeScope.You
    ? { scope: KnowledgeScope.You, area: null }
    : { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, area: null };
}

export function RecordDecision({
  item,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  readonly item: InboxItemDto;
  readonly value: DecisionRecording | null;
  readonly onChange: (next: DecisionRecording | null) => void;
}) {
  const areas = useProjects().data?.find((p) => p.id === item.projectId)?.areas ?? [];
  return (
    <Group gap="sm" align="flex-end">
      <Checkbox
        label="Record as a decision"
        checked={value !== null}
        onChange={(e) => {
          onChange(e.currentTarget.checked ? { scope: KnowledgeScope.Project, area: null } : null);
        }}
      />
      {value !== null ? (
        <Select
          aria-label="Decision applies to"
          size="sm"
          w={220}
          allowDeselect={false}
          data={[
            { value: KnowledgeScope.Project, label: 'This project' },
            { value: KnowledgeScope.You, label: 'All my projects' },
            ...areas.map((a) => ({ value: `${AREA_PREFIX}${a.name}`, label: `Area: ${a.name}` })),
          ]}
          value={valueOf(value)}
          onChange={(next) => {
            if (next !== null) onChange(recordingOf(next));
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
    </Group>
  );
}
```

In `inbox-view.tsx`, the `Draft` gains `readonly record?: DecisionRecording | null;` (absent means the default); compute `const recording = own && own.record !== undefined ? own.record : selected ? defaultRecording(selected) : null;`; and build the answer with it for question cards:

```ts
const trimmed = note.trim();
const base = trimmed === '' ? { choice: picked } : { choice: picked, note: trimmed };
// A question card always says how it is recorded, so the daemon never guesses from the category.
const body = selected.kind === InboxKind.Question ? { ...base, record: recording } : base;
answer.mutate(
  { id, answer: body },
  {
    onSuccess: () => {
      setDraft((current) => (current?.id === id ? null : current));
    },
    onError: (failure) => {
      if (failure instanceof DaemonRequestError && failure.status === 409) {
        patch(id, { error: 'This card was already answered.' });
        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.inbox });
      } else {
        patch(id, { error: failure.message });
      }
    },
  },
);
```

This replaces the `const trimmed …` line and the `answer.mutate(…)` call at the end of `submit`; the callbacks are today's. Pass `recording` and `onRecord={(next) => { patch(item.id, { record: next }); }}` to the selected `InboxCard`, which renders `<RecordDecision item={item} value={recording} onChange={onRecord} />` above the note for a question card (`item.kind === InboxKind.Question`). The digit keys keep choosing options: the checkbox and select take focus only when clicked.

test('records, finds, supersedes and traces knowledge, and a reload loses nothing', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto('/board');
  await open(page, 'Knowledge');
  await expectAccessible(page, 'knowledge, principles, dark');

  await page.getByRole('option', { name: 'Policy' }).click();

  await newPrinciple.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }).click();
  await expect(newPrinciple).toBeHidden();
  const principles = page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Principles' });
  await expect(principles.getByText('Never sleep in tests')).toBeVisible();

  // A decision, read back through search, then superseded.

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Record a decision' }).click();
  const record = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Record a decision' });
  await record.getByLabel('Question').fill('Which greeting style?');
  await record.getByLabel('Choice').fill('Howdy');
  await record.getByLabel('Rationale').fill('Friendly');
  await record.getByRole('button', { name: 'Record' }).click();
  await expect(record).toBeHidden();
  await page.getByLabel('Search').fill('greeting style');

  await decisions.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for "Which greeting style?"' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Supersede' }).click();
  const supersede = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Supersede the decision' });
  await supersede.getByLabel('Choice').fill('Hello');
  await supersede.getByRole('button', { name: 'Supersede' }).click();
  await expect(supersede).toBeHidden();
  await expect(decisions.getByText('Hello', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(decisions.getByText('Howdy')).toBeHidden();

    .click();

  await expect(drawer.getByText('You recorded it in 10db')).toBeVisible();

```bash
deadline=$((SECONDS + 300)); while lsof -nP -iTCP:1420 -iTCP:4318 -sTCP:LISTEN >/dev/null; do [ $SECONDS -lt $deadline ] || { echo 'ports still busy after 5 minutes'; exit 1; }; sleep 5; done
```

### Task 15: The architecture section, decisions, open items and the gate

**Files:**

- Create: `docs/architecture/NN-knowledge.md` (`NN` is the next free section number on `main`; `03` if batch delivery's `02` is the latest)
- Modify: `docs/architecture/decisions.md`, `docs/open-items.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the architecture section**

`docs/architecture/NN-knowledge.md`:

```markdown
# NN Knowledge

- **Status:** Locked
- **Scope:** Decisions, principles, the context pack and the capture flows (factory spec §4, §6, §9,
  §10 Knowledge). Facts, the legacy harness import, PDF intake and embeddings are out.
- **Summary:** Decisions and principles target you, a project or an area; their state is derived on
  read; agents only propose principles; supersession is by a fixed key (a principle's topic key and
  target, a decision's explicit link); every write has a source and nothing is deleted. Each
  session gets a small deterministic pack of the policies, heuristics and decisions that apply.

## Where it lives

- `packages/db`: `principles` and `decisions` with tombstones; every full-text query in
  `store/knowledge-search.ts`; `0001_decisions_fts` and `0002_principles_fts` in the baseline.
- `apps/daemon/src/knowledge/`: scope, the packer, proposals and verdicts, answer capture, the
  always/never detector, the override review, provenance, the index health check.
- `apps/daemon/src/briefing/`: assembles the pack and renders it into the bootstrap prompt and the
  post-compaction re-injection.
- `apps/desktop/src/views/knowledge/`: the Knowledge view.

## The context pack

Policies, then heuristics, then decisions, greedily into 1500 estimated tokens. In scope: `you`,
`project`, and `area` for the task's areas and live area claims. Beliefs are not packed and are set
aside first; then the most specific policy or heuristic per topic key. Decisions: area-matched FTS
hits, other hits, area decisions, else the newest. Each injection (a new session, a resumed one, an
emergency compaction) appends `context.packed` with what it used and dropped.

## Capture

- `propose` (kind `principle`) stores a proposed principle, a pending proposal and a card whose
  options pick the scope. Accepting in the card or the Knowledge view confirms it; rejecting
  withdraws it. A second verdict is a 409.
- An answer records a decision by its category's default, or as `record` says.
- "Always"/"never" sentences in an answer's note become principle proposals.
- Two deviations naming a principle within 30 days raise a "review this principle?" card.

## Invariants

- No knowledge write without a `sources` row; no hard delete.
- Search and the pack see only current records; history is for the builder.
- Only the head of a chain (or a proposed principle) can be withdrawn, so one fixed key never has two
  current records.
- At start, both FTS indexes are checked against their tables and rebuilt if they disagree; a
  rebuild that fails is logged and flagged in each project's inbox, never a crash, and the pack then
  carries the newest decisions instead of task matches.
```

## Self-review notes

- **Spec coverage:**
  - Decision 17: scopes (Tasks 1, 4, 5), agents only propose (Task 7: `propose` never confirms; Task 8 and 10: only the builder's verdict confirms), supersession by fixed key (Tasks 3 and 4), provenance on every write (every insert takes a `sourceId`; Task 10 shows it).
  - §4 `principles` columns: Task 2; "status derived on read": Tasks 3 and 4; withdrawn tombstones: Tasks 2–4.
  - §6 `get_context` (Task 6), `find_knowledge` (Tasks 6–7), `record_decision` (Task 7 area check), `propose` (Task 7), `report_deviation` (Task 9).
  - §9 pack order, budget, `budget_used`/`dropped_count`, explicit emptiness and truncation (Tasks 5–6); injection at start and after compaction (Task 6), each recorded as `context.packed` with reason `start`, `resume` or `compaction`; after a handoff the fresh session's bootstrap prompt carries it (Task 6, the same `buildSessionPrompt` path); capture: answers to decisions (Task 8), always/never with a choice of scope (Tasks 7–8), repeat questions (out, open decision 6), override review (Task 9); provenance and tombstones (Tasks 3, 4, 10).
  - §10 Knowledge: Tasks 11–13; bulk confirm belongs to the import plan.
  - §12 knowledge testing: every write test reads back through search; the runtime health check is Task 10 (Task 3 for the repository), and a failed rebuild is a flagged event, never a daemon crash or a failed briefing (Tasks 6 and 10).
  - §13 item 4: the context pack, the decisions and principles UI and the capture flows; the import and §1.3 on the pilot project are named as later work in the header and in Task 15.
- **Placeholders:** none. Task 15's `NN` is the next free section number, chosen at merge, like the decision numbers (86 onward after main's 69, M2b Part A's 70–76 and batch delivery's 77–85). Commit commands show only the subject; each implementer adds its own session's attribution footer (Global Constraints).
- **Types used across tasks:**
  - `KnowledgeTarget`, `targetColumns`, `TopicKey`, `topicKeyFrom`, `PrincipleDraft`, `ProposalAccept`, `PRINCIPLE_REVIEW_DETAIL`: Task 1. Used in Tasks 4, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12.
  - `listDecisions`, `searchDecisionIds`, `supersedeDecision`, `withdrawDecision`, `checkKnowledgeIndex`: Task 3. Used in Tasks 6, 10.
  - `insertPrinciple`, `confirmPrinciple`, `retirePrinciple`, `withdrawPrinciple`, `listPrinciples`, `searchPrinciples`, `getProposalDto`, `findOpenProposalCard`, `recentDeviationsFor`: Task 4. Used in Tasks 6–10.
  - `packKnowledge`, `mostSpecific`, `PACK_SEARCH_LIMIT`, `CONTEXT_PACK_BUDGET_TOKENS`: Task 5. Used in Tasks 6 and 9.
  - `workstreamAreas`, `decisionHit`, `principleHit`, `contextPackedEvent`, `PackReason`: Task 6. Used in Tasks 7 and 9.
  - `proposePrinciple`: Task 7. Used in Tasks 8 and 10. Its file-private helpers (`VERDICT_FOR_SCOPE`, `draftTarget`, `principleCardOptions`, `placeWords`) are used by Task 8's additions to the same file.
  - `acceptProposal`, `rejectProposal`, `closeProposalCard`, `applyProposalCard`, `recordAnswerDecision`, `proposeStandingRules`, `checkTarget` (`knowledge/targets.ts`): Task 8. Used in Task 10.
  - `repairKnowledgeIndex`, `brokenIndexFlag`: Task 10, used by `daemon/start.ts` in the same task.
  - Exports stay where a caller is: `knowledge/index.ts` re-exports only what another daemon module imports, and a helper used only in its own file is not exported (knip, Task 15).

- **Review Focus coverage:** each of the six items names its tests, and each named test is written out in its task.
